七座位面 + 逆相终局

Seven realms + inverted rift

交汇枢纽 · 灰白森林 · 魔法遗境 · 米勒荒原 · 破碎时线 · 混沌边境 · 烬火之上 —— 七座位面环而成环，终局在逆相里等你选。

Intersection Hub · Grey Wood · Magic Realm · Miller’s Wastes · Broken Timeline · Chaos Line · Embers End — seven realms in one ring; the finale waits in the Upside breach.

五场 Boss 大战

Five boss duels

影界守护者 · 大奥术师萨尔林 · 引力化身奇点领主 · 时线执政官 · 混沌裂隙领主。锁定 → 弹反 → 反手，每尊都要十二遍手感。

Shadow Warden · Archmagus Sarlin · Singularity Herald · Timeline Inspector · Chaos Overlord. Lock-on, parry, punish — each takes a dozen goes to learn.

终章选择

The ending choice

两个都对、都有意义：留灯 · 把火交给后来人；安静走完 · 把旅程收好。「炉主」成就 + 出场页金字 = 已经走完这一趟的人会被看见。

Both answers are right and both are heavy: keep the flame / gather the journey. “Echo-keeper” — the start page burns a gold pill for those who walk to the end.

自动通关 Demo

Autoplay demo

按 T — 游戏自己走到结局。可用于剧情鉴赏 / 性能分享 / CI 门禁（e2e 通关全绿）。

Press T — the game walks itself to the ending. Story viewing, performance posts — and the repo’s own e2e gate clears the game hands-off.

工程与架构EngineeringTHE REAL STACK

九个微服务（Spring Cloud）

Nine Spring Cloud services

Eureka · Config · Gateway · game · season · ai · presence · notification (8085) — LB + Circuit Breaker + Retry + TimeLimiter 全部接通，9 模块 verify 全绿。

Eureka · Config · Gateway · game · season · ai · presence · notification (8085) — LB + Circuit Breaker + Retry + TimeLimiter everywhere; all nine modules verify green.

Redis 全矩阵

Redis everywhere

存在TTL · 会榜 ZSET · 赛季计数 · 报讯台List/Hash/PubSub · AI队列 Streams · 幂等跨实例台账（新）。mirror-write 模式一致。

Presence TTL · leaderboard ZSETs · season counters · notification List/Hash/PubSub · AI job Streams · cross-instance idempotency ledger (new). All mirror-write.

缓存体系

Cache theory

堆顶K+解耦 memo · Cache-Control 全路径 · LRU · TTL · Spring Cache（simple/redis）—— 三层缓存模型，主线 TTL 都在。

Heap-K + decoupled memo · Cache-Control per path · LRU · TTL · Spring Cache (simple/redis) — three tiers, all TTL-bounded.

可观测性入口

Ops observability

/api/ops/health· notifyMirrors 计数 · /api/ops/redis· /api/ops/advisor· /api/ops/dead-letter· /actuator/springcloud—— 每一步都可被钩子验。

/api/ops/health · notifyMirrors counters · /api/ops/redis · /api/ops/advisor · /api/ops/dead-letter · /actuator/springcloud — every path hook-verified.

11 · 16Oracle prompt-cache 分桶实证 + 冻结修复 · W-Z247 线上 gpt-6.1-terra 实测发现：canon lane 两次同问 cached_tokens=null（0%）——冻结的 lore-system-prompt ≈500 token，低于上游 prompt-cache 的 ~1024-token 页门槛；refs lane excerpt 够大命中 96.9%。修：从 creative/GLOSSARY.md 首 ~2500 字节原样摘取为 lore-digest-frozen.txt 并入 system（build-freeze-glossary.mjs 确定性生成、byte-identical）——canon #2 → cached=6784/6973 ≈97.3% ✓；Java live 3/3 绿（refs 命中 refs 卷、canon 命中 canon 卷、无义拒答 latency=0）。新增 scripts/oracle-cache-probe.mjs 双轨实证探针。W-Z247 — live gpt-6.1-terra measurement showed the canon lane never hit prompt-cache: two identical asks both returned cached_tokens=null, because the frozen lore-system-prompt (~500 tokens) sits below the upstream ~1024-token page threshold, while the refs excerpt alone crossed it (96.9% hit). Fix: prepend a verbatim ~2500-byte digest of creative/GLOSSARY.md head (scripts/build-freeze-glossary.mjs, byte-identical, deterministic) as a frozen system block — canon #2 → cached_tokens=6784/6973 ≈ 97.3% ✓; Java live tests 3/3 green (refs → refs volume, canon → novel sources, refusal → latency=0, zero tokens). New probe scripts/oracle-cache-probe.mjs for per-lane measurement.

11 · 16第一百一十八卷入典 · W-Z267 · 卷十五之十九 · 一勺汤 《一勺汤》——雨停了七天——老李连着来了七天——第六天他把一个白布扎成的小包放在灶台上——母亲打开——里面是一块白布——布上用针脚缝了一个很淡的火苗——「我来学添柴」——「我不要名字」——阿石端出一碗饭——他盯着灶火看了很长——他说「七百年没看见的火——这两天才开始看见——它呼吸」——母亲说「来」——七百年第一次——有人在灶前学添柴。Vol.118 · The Spoonful of Soup — on the sixth day after the rain finally broke, Old Li laid a small cloth bundle on the stove, inside a tiny flame stitched in thread; on the seventh day he came again and went straight to the stove — picked up an old wooden spoon — ladled one spoonful of hot broth into his bowl and drank; “I’ve come to learn how to feed the fire,” he said; “I don’t want a title”; A-Shi brought a bowl of rice; Old Li stared at the stove for a long time; “Seven hundred years,” he said slowly, “I’ve been looking at the wrong fire this whole time; these days I’m finally beginning to see it properly; it breathes”; his mother said only: “Come.”
11 · 16第一百一十七卷入典 · W-Z266 · 卷十五之十八 · 隔壁的灶 《隔壁的灶》——两周来的雨没歇——隔壁老李每天都来——他先站半个钟头——第二天站整个上午——第三天往灶台前又挪近一点——第四天下雨——他鞋全湿了——他说「我只一个人来——不求全合一起」——第十天天放晴了——他端一个小锅——蹲在灶前——只放了三四粒米——锅里水起翻动的声音——他出去站廊下——只说一句「炉火——明天我还来」——风吹开院门——全家人站在廊下看着——灶火没有灭。Vol.117 · The Neighbor’s Stove — the rain at the neighbor’s house had not let up; Old Li came every day — half an hour the first day, the whole morning the second, then one foot from the stove, then bare wet shoes; “I came for the fire — just me, not my six”; on the tenth day it cleared; Old Li came carrying a small iron pot, crouched by the stove, and dropped three or four grains of rice into a splash of water; the pot stirred — barely a sound; he went out to the porch: “Stove, I’ll come again tomorrow”; no one answered; the wind pushed the gate open; the family stood on the porch and watched — the stove had not gone out; for the first time in seven hundred years, this household and the neighbor had waited together at one hearth.

11 · 16第一百一十六卷入典 · W-Z264 · 卷十五之十七 · 炉火的下一次 《炉火的下一次》——立春后第十二天——早上来了第一个客人——不是老人——是隔壁的老李——他家六口人——自己的灶台不行——他搓着手说「他们家的炉火不灵——我们想叫那炉火给咱们壮一壮胆」——第二天一场雨——老李湿着一半身子站在廊檐下——他没敢进灶间——阿石端两碗面出来——老李吸着鼻子差点说话——只说了一句「我回去再来」——母亲把他的碗洗了翻过来——灶火今晚没有灭——七百年第一次——隔壁的灶和自家的灶在一张桌上吃过饭。Vol.116 · The Next Visit — the first guest of spring arrives twelve days after the equinox; it is not the old man from the mountain but the neighbor, old Li, whose stove at home has never burned as steady as his; “yours is alive, ours isn’t”; A-Shi doesn’t contradict him; “if it’s all right with you, you can come see it tomorrow”; the next morning a real rain rolls in and Old Li stands under the eave with one sleeve already wet; he won’t come inside; A-Shi brings two bowls to the porch; Old Li inhales once, barely able to speak; he says: “I’ll come back tomorrow”; the dishes are washed when he leaves; the stove does not go out that night; seven hundred years, and the neighbor’s hearth has finally shared a table with this one.
11 · 19第二百四十一卷入典 · W-Z438 · 卷二十之三十一 · 柴 《柴》——第七百零三年——立春后第二十五周——周日——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——柴堆少了——他出去了——他拿了一把柴——他放进灶里——灶火燃着——他放了第二把——灶火燃着——他放了第三把——灶火燃着——母亲说「——柴够了——」——阿石说「——柴够了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.241 · The Firewood — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox — Sunday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the firewood pile was low — he went out — he picked up an armful of wood — he placed it into the hearth — the fire burned — he placed a second armful — the fire burned — he placed a third — the fire burned — his mother said: “ — enough —”; A-Shi said: “ — enough —”. Volume XX, thirty-first part.
11 · 19第二百四十卷入典 · W-Z437 · 卷二十之三十 · 灶灰的来信 《灶灰的来信》——第七百零三年——立春后第二十四周——周六——下午——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——他拿了一张旧纸——他把它放在桌上——他说「——看——」——母亲说「——看——」——阿石看了——纸上有字——字是淡色的——阿石说「——淡了——」——母亲说「——淡了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.240 · The Ash Letter — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twenty-fourth week after the spring equinox — Saturday — afternoon — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — he took out an old piece of paper — he placed it on the table — he said: “ — look —”; his mother said: “ — look —” — there were characters on the page — the characters were faint — he said: “ — they have faded —”; his mother said: “ — they have faded —”. Volume XX, thirtieth part.
11 · 19第二百三十九卷入典 · W-Z436 · 卷二十之二十九 · 灶灰的影子 《灶灰的影子》——第七百零三年——立春后第二十三周——周五——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——他拿了一把灰——他洒在泥地上——他说「——洒——」——他说「——影——」——母亲说「——影——」——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着。Vol.239 · The Shadow of the Ash — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twenty-third week after the spring equinox — Friday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — he took a handful of ash — he scattered it across the earthen floor — he said: “ — scatter —” — he said: “ — the shadow —”; his mother said: “ — the shadow —” — the fire burned. Volume XX, twenty-ninth part.
11 · 19第二百三十八卷入典 · W-Z435 · 卷二十之二十八 · 客栈 《客栈》——第七百零三年——立春后第二十二周——周四——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——西风停了——他拿起扫帚——他从门口扫到厨桌——他说「——扫——」——母亲说「——扫——」——阿石扫完了——地板上——有白桦叶——他捡起一片——他说「——白桦叶——」——母亲说「——白桦叶——」——他把白桦叶放进灶里——灶火燃着——他说「——白桦进了灶——」——母亲说「——白桦进了灶——」——灶火燃着。Vol.238 · The Inn — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twenty-second week after the spring equinox — Thursday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the west wind had stopped — he took up the broom — he swept from the door to the kitchen table — he said: “ — sweep —”; his mother said: “ — sweep —” — on the floor — there was a birch leaf — he picked it up — he said: “ — a birch leaf —”; his mother said: “ — a birch leaf —” — he placed the birch leaf into the hearth — he said: “ — the birch went into the hearth —”; his mother said: “ — the birch went into the hearth —”. Volume XX, twenty-eighth part.
11 · 19第二百三十七卷入典 · W-Z434 · 卷二十之二十七 · 风铃 《风铃》——第七百零三年——立春后第二十一周——周三——下午——灶火燃着——阿石走出巷口——西边来了一个人——那个人穿着旅人的斗篷——斗篷上有尘土——他推开门——他说「——客栈——」——掌柜的说「——客栈——」——掌柜的说「——粥好了——」——旅人吃了一口——旅人说「——西风——带着白桦的味道——」——阿石说「——白桦——」——旅人说「——我见过锚火——它不远了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.237 · The Wind Bell — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twenty-first week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — afternoon — the fire burned; A-Shi walked out of the lane — from the west a man came — the man wore a traveler’s cloak — the cloak was covered in dust — he pushed open the door — he said: “ — inn —” — the innkeeper said: “ — inn —” — the traveler said: “ — the west wind — it carries the smell of birch —” — the traveler said: “ — I have seen it — it is not far now —”. Volume XX, twenty-seventh part.
11 · 19第二百三十六卷入典 · W-Z432 · 卷二十之二十五 · 烬 《烬》——第七百零三年——立春后第二十周——周五——夜里——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——灶肚子里——只剩一圈灰——灰是红色的——中间——一颗——红得发亮——阿石说「——烬——」——母亲说「——烬——」——他说「——还亮——」——母亲说「——还亮——」——灶火燃着——他看着——亮着——灶火燃着。Vol.236 · The Ember — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twentieth week after the spring equinox — Friday — night — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — inside the belly of the hearth — there was nothing but a ring of ash — the ash was red at the edge — in the middle — one coal — glowing — he said: “ — an ember —”; his mother said: “ — an ember —” — he said: “ — still glowing —”; his mother said: “ — still glowing —”. Volume XX, twenty-fifth part.
11 · 19第二百三十五卷入典 · W-Z430 · 卷二十之二十四 · 灯芯 《灯芯》——第七百零三年——立春后第十九周——周四——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——夜深了——灶肚子里——灶火燃着——她把灯芯挑了一挑——灯亮了——阿石说「——挑灯芯——」——母亲说「——挑灯芯——」——阿石看着——灯又亮了——他说「——我看书——」——母亲说「——你看书——」——灶火燃着——灯亮——灶火燃着。Vol.235 · The Wick — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the nineteenth week after the spring equinox — Thursday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the night had deepened — she trimmed the wick — the lamp brightened — A-Shi said: “ — trim the wick —”; his mother said: “ — trim the wick —” — the lamp grew brighter — he said: “ — I am going to read —”; his mother said: “ — you are going to read —” — the fire burned. Volume XX, twenty-fourth part.
11 · 19第二百三十四卷入典 · W-Z429 · 卷二十之二十三 · 添柴 《添柴》——第七百零三年——立春后第十八周——周三——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——灶火小——灶肚子里——灰也少了——他把树枝拿过来——他拣了三根——他把第一根——放进灶里——灶火燃着——他说「——添柴——」——母亲说「——添柴——」——他说「——大了——」——母亲说「——大了——」——第三根——灶火燃着。Vol.234 · The Kindle — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the eighteenth week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the fire was low — he brought the branches over — he picked three — he placed the first branch — into the hearth — the flame grew — he said: “ — feed the fire —”; his mother said: “ — feed the fire —” — the fire burned. Volume XX, twenty-third part.
11 · 19第二百三十三卷入典 · W-Z428 · 卷二十之二十二 · 灶灰 《灶灰》——第七百零三年——立春后第十七周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——灶火小——灶肚子里——灶灰厚了——他说「——要清了——」——母亲说「——要清了——」——阿石拿了扫帚——他扫了一遍——灶灰少了——他说「——好了——」——母亲说「——好了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.233 · The Hearth Ash — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the seventeenth week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the ash in the belly of the hearth had thickened — he said: “ — it is time to sweep —”; his mother said: “ — it is time to sweep —” — A-Shi took a broom — he swept — the ash was gone — he said: “ — it is done —”. Volume XX, twenty-second part.
11 · 19第二百三十二卷入典 · W-Z427 · 卷二十之二十一 · 破壳 《破壳》——第七百零三年——立春后第十六周——周一——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——馒头还热——阿石拿起一个——他看见了馒头的壳——壳裂着——他说「——破壳——」——母亲说「——破壳——」——阿石把馒头掰开——里面是白的——他把一半给了母亲——灶火燃着。Vol.232 · The Split — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the sixteenth week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the steamed buns were still hot — A-Shi took one up — he saw the shell was cracked — he said: “ — the shell split —”; his mother said: “ — the shell split —” — A-Shi pulled the bun apart — inside it was white — he gave half to his mother — she said: “ — it tastes good —”. Volume XX, twenty-first part.
11 · 19第二百三十一卷入典 · W-Z426 · 卷二十之二十 · 热气 《热气》——第七百零三年——立春后第十五周——周日——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——馒头还热——他拿起一个——他咬了一口——烫——他等了一等——他又咬了一口——刚好——灶火燃着——他把馒头掰成两半——给了母亲一半——灶火燃着。Vol.231 · The Steam Heat — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the fifteenth week after the spring equinox — Sunday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the steamed buns were still hot — he took one up — he bit into it — he split the bun in two — he gave half to his mother. Volume XX, twentieth part.
11 · 19第二百三十卷入典 · W-Z425 · 卷二十之十九 · 蒸 《蒸》——第七百零三年——立春后第十四周——周六——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——母亲已经在灶前——水开了——她把馒头胚放进甑——盖上——阿石闻——有面香——她揭开甑——她说「——好了——」——阿石说「——好了——」——馒头出锅了——灶火燃着。Vol.230 · The Steam — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the fourteenth week after the spring equinox — Saturday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — his mother was already at the fire — the water had boiled — she placed the buns into the steamer — she covered it — A-Shi smelled — there was the scent of dough — she lifted the lid — she said: “ — ready —” — A-Shi said: “ — ready —” — the buns came out of the steamer. Volume XX, nineteenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十九卷入典 · W-Z424 · 卷二十之十八 · 面团 《面团》——第七百零三年——立春后第十三周——周五——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——母亲已经把麦粒粉搬到厨桌上——她加水——她加一点盐——她开始揉——粉成了面——她说「——面团——」——母亲说「——面团——」——她把面团盖上——她说「——等一等——」——阿石说「——等一等——」——她说「——等一等——」——灶火燃着。Vol.229 · The Dough — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the thirteenth week after the spring equinox — Friday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — his mother poured the flour into the bowl — she added water — she added a little salt — she began to knead — the flour became dough — she covered the dough — she said: “ — wait a while —”. Volume XX, eighteenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十八卷入典 · W-Z423 · 卷二十之十七 · 磨 《磨》——第七百零三年——立春后第十二周——周四——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——他把麦穗搬到磨坊——老磨坊——墙是石头——他把麦穗倒进石臼——他开始磨——很慢——手都是麦粒粉——他说「——磨——」——母亲说「——磨——」——他又磨——灶火燃着——地上有麦粒粉——灶火燃着。Vol.228 · The Mill — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the twelfth week after the spring equinox — Thursday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — he carried the ears to the mill — the old mill — stone walls — he poured the ears into the stone mortar — he began to mill — it was very slow — his hands were covered in flour — he said: “ — the mill —”; his mother said: “ — the mill —” — the fire burned. Volume XX, seventeenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十七卷入典 · W-Z422 · 卷二十之十六 · 囤 《囤》——第七百零三年——立春后第十一周——周三——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——阿石把麦穗扛回来——堂屋放不下了——他把麦穗铺在堂屋——地上——一排——两排——三排——他说「——囤——」——母亲说「——囤——」——阿石的额头上有汗——母亲给他递了一杯水——他说「——多得很——」——母亲说「——多得很——」——灶火燃着。Vol.227 · The Silo — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the eleventh week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — he carried the ears in — he spread them across the floor — one row — two rows — three rows — he said: “ — the silo —”; his mother said: “ — the silo —” — his mother handed him a cup of water — he said: “ — it is a great many —”. Volume XX, sixteenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十六卷入典 · W-Z421 · 卷二十之十五 · 收割 《收割》——第七百零三年——立春后第十周——周六——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——麦穗黄了——老麦穗——风吹——风过——麦秆动——他拿出镰刀——他说「——收割——」——母亲说「——收割——」——他蹲下去——他割——第一镰——麦穗落——第二镰——麦穗落——一镰——一镰——灶火燃着——他把麦穗抱回家——灶火燃着。Vol.226 · The Harvest — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the tenth week after the spring equinox — Saturday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the ears had turned yellow — old ears — he brought out the sickle — he said: “ — the harvest —”; his mother said: “ — the harvest —” — he squatted down — he cut — the first stroke — the ears fell — the second stroke — the ears fell — the fire burned — he carried the ears home — the fire burned. Volume XX, fifteenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十五卷入典 · W-Z420 · 卷二十之十四 · 麦浪 《麦浪》——第七百零三年——立春后第九周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——风从田那边来——风起——风过——麦穗全动了——麦穗像水——像一片海——他看——他说「——麦浪——」——母亲说「——麦浪——」——风又来了——麦穗摇——他说「——一样——」——母亲说「——一样——」——灶火燃着。Vol.225 · The Wheat Sea — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the ninth week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the wind came from the fields — the ears were like water — like a sea — he said: “ — the wheat sea —”; his mother said: “ — the wheat sea —” — the wind came again — the ears swayed — he said: “ — the same —”. Volume XX, fourteenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十四卷入典 · W-Z419 · 卷二十之十三 · 灌浆 《灌浆》——第七百零三年——立春后第八周——周一——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——穗子往下弯——他摸了一下麦粒——麦粒硬了——他说「——灌浆——」——母亲说「——灌浆——」——他蹲下去——他说「——硬了——」——母亲说「——硬了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.224 · The Grain Filling — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the eighth week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the ears were bending down — he touched a grain — the grain had hardened — he said: “ — the grain is filling —”; his mother said: “ — the grain is filling —” — he said: “ — they are hard —”. Volume XX, thirteenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十三卷入典 · W-Z418 · 卷二十之十二 · 扬花 《扬花》——第七百零三年——立春后第七周——周日——下午——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——田里面青色的——风轻——风来——麦穗在风里摆——摆开了——他说「——扬花——」——母亲说「——扬花——」——风停了——他说「——了——」——母亲说「——了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.223 · The Pollination — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the seventh week after the spring equinox — Sunday — afternoon — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the wind was light — the ears of wheat swayed in the wind — he said: “ — the pollination —”; his mother said: “ — the pollination —” — the wind stopped — he said: “ — it is done —”. Volume XX, twelfth part.
11 · 19第二百二十二卷入典 · W-Z414 · 卷二十之十一 · 抽穗 《抽穗》——第七百零三年——立春后第六周——周六——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——田里面青色的——有几分高了——他蹲下去——他摸了一下麦苗——籽粒还在叶鞘里——他说「——抽穗——」——母亲说「——抽穗——」——他把手掌放在穗子上——他说「——快了——」——母亲说「——快了——」——灶火燃着。Vol.222 · The Ears of Wheat — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the sixth week after the spring equinox — Saturday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the fields were green — taller already — he squatted down — he touched the shoots — the grains were still inside the sheath — he said: “ — they are coming out of the sheath —”; his mother said: “ — they are coming out of the sheath —” — he rested his palm over the heads — he said: “ — soon —”. Volume XX, eleventh part.
11 · 19第二百二十一卷入典 · W-Z413 · 卷二十之十 · 麦粒 《麦粒》——第七百零三年——立春后第五周——周一——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——田里面青色的——有几分高——比筷子高——他蹲下去——他摸了一把——他拿起一颗麦粒——很轻——他说「——麦粒——」——母亲说「——麦粒——」——他把麦粒放进口袋——他说「——种子——」——母亲说「——种子——」——灶火燃着。Vol.221 · The Wheat Grain — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the fifth week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the fields were green — taller than chopsticks — he squatted down — he ran his hand through — he picked up a wheat grain — it was very light — he said: “ — grain —”; his mother said: “ — grain —” — he put it in his pocket — he said: “ — a seed —”. Volume XX, tenth part.
11 · 19第二百二十卷入典 · W-Z411 · 卷二十之九 · 谷雨 《谷雨》——第七百零三年——立春后第四周——周五——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——天刚亮——天还灰——他听——有响声——是雨——落在白桦的叶子上——比针还细——他伸手——他接了几滴——他把手放下来——他说「——谷雨——」——母亲说「——谷雨——」——灶火燃着。Vol.220 · Grain Rain — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the fourth week after the spring equinox — Friday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the sky was just brightening — the sky was still grey — he listened — there was a faint sound — it was rain — falling on the birch leaves — finer than needles — he reached out — he caught a few drops — he lowered his hand — he said: “ — grain rain —”; his mother said: “ — grain rain —”. Volume XX, ninth part.
11 · 18第二百一十九卷入典 · W-Z409 · 卷二十之八 · 白桦吻 《白桦吻》——第七百零三年——立春后第三周——周三——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——白桦的叶子全是绿的——风轻——他伸手——他摸了一下白桦的叶子——它很轻——母亲站在厨桌前——她看他——她没开口——白桦吻——灶火燃着。Vol.219 · The Birch’s Kiss — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the third week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the birch was fully green — the wind was light — he reached out — he touched a birch leaf — his mother stood at the kitchen table — she watched him — she did not speak. Volume XX, eighth part.
11 · 18第二百一十八卷入典 · W-Z408 · 卷二十之七 · 夏火 《夏火》——第七百零三年——立春后第二周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——白桦的叶子全出来了——比拇指大两倍——风轻——太阳出来——母亲说「——夏火——」——阿石说「——夏火——」——灶火燃着——母亲说「——灶火要守——」——阿石说「——灶火要守——」——夏火——灶火燃着。Vol.218 · Summer Fire — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the second week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the birch leaves were fully out — twice the size of a thumb — the wind was light — sunlight came out — his mother said: “ — summer fire —”; A-Shi said: “ — summer fire —”; his mother said: “ — the fire must be kept —”. Volume XX, seventh part.
11 · 18第二百一十七卷入典 · W-Z407 · 卷二十之六 · 灯灭了 《灯灭了》——第七百零三年——立春后第一周——周六——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石坐在灶前——煤油灯亮着——夜里没有灯——夜里没有风——阿石说「——灶火——」——母亲说「——守着——」——他没添柴——火小了——灯灭了——灶火燃着——他记住——灶火就——这点事——灯灭了——灶火燃着。Vol.217 · The Lamp Went Out — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the first week after the spring equinox — Saturday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi sat before the stove — the oil lamp was lit — no light at night — no wind at night — A-Shi said: “ — the fire —”; his mother said: “ — keep it —”; he did not add wood — the flame grew small — the lamp went out — the fire burned. Volume XX, sixth part.
11 · 19第二百一十六卷入典 · W-Z406 · 卷二十之五 · 春天 《春天》——第七百零三年——立春后第一周——周五——早上——灶火燃着——阿石站在门口——白桦的芽叶出来了——比拇指大一点——母亲站在厨桌前——她没说话——阿石说「——春天——」——母亲说「——春天——」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——灶火要守——」——母亲说「——灶火要守——」——春天——灶火燃着。Vol.216 · Spring — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the first week after the spring equinox — Friday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood at the doorway — the birch had sprouted leaves — they were about the size of a thumb — his mother stood at the kitchen table — she did not speak — A-Shi said: “ — spring —”; his mother said: “ — spring —”. Volume XX, fifth part.

11 · 18第二百一十五卷入典 · W-Z404 · 卷二十之四 · 火候 《火候》——第七百零三年——立春后第一周——周四——早上——灶火燃着——阿石烧了一碗粥——它还没到点——母亲说「——再等——」——灶火燃着——粥好了——火候——灶火燃着。Vol.215 · The Right Heat — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the first week after the spring equinox — Thursday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi was cooking a bowl of porridge — it had not reached the point — his mother said: “ — wait —” — the porridge was done — the right heat. Volume XX, fourth part.
11 · 18第二百一十四卷入典 · W-Z403 · 卷二十之三 · 薪火 《薪火》——第七百零三年——立春后第一周——周三——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石坐在灶前——他看着火——他说「——薪火——」——母亲说「——薪火——」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——守火——」——母亲说「——守火——」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——我记住——」——母亲说「——你记住——」——薪火——灶火燃着。Vol.214 · The Kindle — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the first week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi sat before the stove — he watched the flame — he said: “ — the kindle —”; his mother said: “ — the kindle —”; A-Shi said: “ — keeping the fire —”; his mother said: “ — keeping the fire —”; A-Shi said: “ — I remember —”; his mother said: “ — you remember —”. Volume XX, third part.
11 · 18第二百一十三卷入典 · W-Z402 · 卷二十之二 · 灯下有灰 《灯下有灰》——第七百零三年——立春后第一周——周二——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石点上了灯——灯照见灶膛——灰落在他手上——他看——他没抖——母亲说「——灰落在掌上——手不抖——」——阿石说「——不抖——」——母亲说「——守火的手——」——灯下有灰——灶火燃着。Vol.213 · Ash Beneath the Lamp — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the first week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi lit the lamp — the lamp showed the stove’s mouth — ash fell onto his hand — he watched — the hand did not shake — his mother said: “ — ash on the palm — the hand does not shake —”; his mother said: “ — the keeper’s hand —”. Volume XX, second part.
11 · 18第二百一十二卷入典 · W-Z401 · 卷二十卷一 · 灶堂的灰 《灶堂的灰》——第七百零三年——立春后第一周——周一——早上——灶火燃着——阿石扫灶——灰很细——他把灰拢到簸箕——他不知道——往哪里倒灰——母亲说「——灶堂里——倒进去——」——阿石说「——灶堂里——」——母亲说「——灶堂里——」——灶火燃着。卷二十开篇。Vol.212 · The Ash of the Stove — the seven-hundred-and-third year — the first week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi swept the stove — the ash was fine — he gathered the ash into a pan — he did not know where to pour the ash — his mother said: “ — into the stove chamber —”. Volume XX, first part — volume XX opens.
11 · 18第二百一十一卷入典 · W-Z400 · 卷十九之十六 · 他听见了 《他听见了》——立春后第十三周——周三——早上——灶火燃着——阿石守在灶前——眼睛睁着——他没睡——灶火燃着——他听到了——灶火燃着——那种声音——比普通的火更轻——比普通的火更远——阿石说「——我听——」——灶火燃着——他听见了——灶火燃着。Vol.211 · He Heard It — the thirteenth week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi kept the stove — his eyes were open — he did not sleep; he heard it — that sound — lighter than an ordinary fire — farther than an ordinary fire — A-Shi said: “ — I am listening —”; he heard it. Volume XIX, sixteenth part.
11 · 18第二百一十卷入典 · W-Z399 · 卷十九之十五 · 他说了 《他说了》——立春后第十三周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——白桦响了一声——比平时更响——母亲站在门边——她没有说——阿石说「——谁——」——母亲说「——阿祖——」——阿石说「——阿祖——」——母亲说「——他说守火的人——不要停——」——阿石说「——不停——」——母亲说「——不停——」——灶火燃着——他说了——灶火燃着。Vol.210 · He Said — the thirteenth week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; the birch sounded — once — louder than usual — his mother stood at the door — she did not speak — A-Shi said: “ — who —”; his mother said: “ — Azu —”; his mother said: “ — he said those who keep the fire — must not stop —”; A-Shi said: “ — not stop —”. Volume XIX, fifteenth part.
11 · 18第二百零九卷入典 · W-Z398 · 卷十九之十四 · 谢客 《谢客》——立春后第十二周——周一——早上——灶火燃着——门响了一声——仆人阿金说「——有客——」——阿石去开门——门外——风大——一个老人站着——他手里什么都没拿——灶火燃着——老人说「——灶火还在——」——阿石说「——还在——」——老人说「——叫你说了——」——阿石说「——说了——」——灶火燃着——老人走了——阿金说「——这人——谁也不认识——」——阿石说「——灶火知道——」——谢客——灶火燃着。Vol.209 · The Thanked Guest — the twelfth week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the fire burned; the door sounded — Ah-Jin said: “ — a guest —” — A-Shi opened it — outside the wind was hard — an old man stood — his hands were empty — the old man said: “ — the fire is still alive —”; A-Shi said: “ — it is —”; Ah-Jin said: “ — nobody knows him —”; A-Shi said: “ — the fire knows —”. Volume XIX, fourteenth part.
11 · 18第二百零八卷入典 · W-Z397 · 卷十九之十三 · 守夜的冬 《守夜的冬》——立春后第十一周——周五——早上——灶火燃着——阿石醒来——窗外有霜——他没开门——他先看灶火——他添了柴——火燃起来了——母亲做早饭——她听见他起来了——阿石说「——霜——」——母亲说「——守夜的冬——会过——」——阿石说「——会过——」——灶火燃着。Vol.208 · The Winter of the Vigil — the eleventh week after the spring equinox — Friday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi woke — there was frost on the window — he did not open the door — he looked at the fire first — he added wood — the flame rose; his mother said: “ — the winter of the vigil will pass —”; A-Shi said: “ — it will pass —”. Volume XIX, thirteenth part.
11 · 18第二百零七卷入典 · W-Z396 · 卷十九之十二 · 谢火 《谢火》——立春后第十周——周四——早上——灶火燃着——阿石添了柴——火燃起来了——母亲说「——谢谢它——」——阿石说「——谢谢它——」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——谢——」——母亲说「——灶火在听——它知道的——」——谢火——灶火燃着。Vol.207 · Thanking the Fire — the tenth week after the spring equinox — Thursday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi added wood — the flame rose — his mother said: “ — thank it —”; A-Shi said: “ — thank you —”; his mother said: “ — the fire is listening — it knows —”. Volume XIX, twelfth part.
11 · 18第二百零六卷入典 · W-Z395 · 卷十九之十一 · 守夜的夜 《守夜的夜》——立春后第十周——周三——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石一宿没睡——守夜——煤油灯没点——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——天亮了——阿石说「——守住了——」——母亲说「——守住了——」——守夜的夜——灶火燃着。Vol.206 · The Night of the Vigil — the tenth week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi did not sleep all night — he kept the vigil — he did not light the oil lamp — the fire burned — the fire burned — the fire burned — the sky brightened — A-Shi said: “ — I kept it —”; his mother said: “ — you kept it —”. Volume XIX, eleventh part.
11 · 18第二百零五卷入典 · W-Z394 · 卷十九之十 · 应声 《应声》——立春后第九周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——阿石劈柴——斧头落下——柴开了——他听见一声闷响——像是有人在屋里说了一句什么——但很轻——母亲说「——守火的——要认识柴——灶火要的不是最大的一根——是对的那一根——」——阿石说「——喂——」——灶火燃着——母亲说「——它会应你——前提是——你没骗它——」——阿石说「——我没骗它——」——母亲说「——那它会应——」——应声——灶火燃着。Vol.205 · The Answering Voice — the ninth week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi split wood — the axe fell — the wood split — he heard a dull sound — as if someone in the room had said something — but very soft — his mother said: “ — a keeper of the fire — must know wood — the fire does not want the biggest piece — but the right one —”; A-Shi said: “ — hey —”; his mother said: “ — it will answer you — but only if you have not lied to it —”; A-Shi said: “ — I have not lied to it —”; his mother said: “ — then it will answer —”. Volume XIX, tenth part.
11 · 18第二百零四卷入典 · W-Z393 · 卷十九之九 · 风 《风》——立春后第九周——周一——早上——灶火燃着——阿石开了窗——风进来了——风带了些东西——泥——雪——花粉——什么的——母亲说「——春天——」——阿石说「——春天——」——灶火燃着——风去了——灶火燃着。Vol.204 · Wind — the ninth week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi opened the window — the wind came in — the wind brought things — clay — snow — pollen — his mother said: “ — spring —”; A-Shi said: “ — spring —”; the wind was gone; the fire burned. Volume XIX, ninth part.
11 · 18第二百零三卷入典 · W-Z392 · 卷十九之八 · 烟火 《烟火》——立春后第八周——周三——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石屋里点了烟——火渐渐暗——他没开灯——灶火燃着——烟袅起来——他看——灶火燃着——母亲说「——北风要来了——烟会往西——」——阿石说「——烟会往西——」——母亲说「——灶火的烟——每天往一个方向——」——烟火——灶火燃着。Vol.203 · Kitchen Smoke — the eighth week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi lit the stove — the flame dimmed — he did not turn on the lamp — the smoke curled up — he watched — his mother said: “ — the north wind is coming — the smoke will lean west —”; his mother said: “ — the smoke of the hearth — each day it leans in one direction —”. Volume XIX, eighth part.
11 · 18第二百零二卷入典 · W-Z391 · 卷十九之七 · 守火之人 《守火之人》——立春后第八周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——阿石出门前——母亲看他——母亲说「——去烧柴——」——阿石说「——柴小的时候——灶火才烧得旺——」——母亲说「——守火的——要认识柴——灶火要的不是最大的一根——是对的那一根——」——阿石说「——对的那一根——」——母亲说「——对的那一根——」——灶火燃着。Vol.202 · The Keeper of the Fire — the eighth week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi stood before leaving — his mother said: “ — go cut wood —”; A-Shi said: “ — when the wood is small — the flame burns well —”; his mother said: “ — a keeper of the fire — must know wood — the fire does not want the biggest piece — but the right one —”. Volume XIX, seventh part.
11 · 18第二百零一卷入典 · W-Z390 · 卷十九之六 · 白昼 《白昼》——立春后第七周——周六——中午——灶火燃着——阿石醒来——窗外的光已经很足——灶火燃着——火还不太旺——够用了——母亲说「——阿祖以前常在白天守火——」——阿石说「——白天——他守谁——」——母亲说「——守下午——」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——守下午——」——母亲说「——守下午——火不会知道——但总要有人——」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——我在——」——母亲说「——我在——」——白昼——灶火燃着。Vol.201 · Daytime — the seventh week after the spring equinox — Saturday — noon — the fire burned; A-Shi woke — the light outside was already full; his mother said: “ — Azu used to keep the fire in the day too —”; A-Shi said: “ — whom did he keep it for —”; his mother said: “ — he kept it for the afternoon —”. Volume XIX, sixth part.
11 · 18第一百九十九→第二百卷入典 · W-Z389 · 卷十九之五 · 补柴（典籍馆 200 卷里程碑） 《补柴》——立春后第七周——周三——下午——灶火燃着——阿石劈柴——柴劈好了一半——他看——灶火燃着——火势稍好——不灭了——母亲说「——修——灶膛裂了一条缝——」——阿石说「——修——」——灶火燃着——阿石拿了泥——他把裂缝糊住——火苗稳了——母亲说「——它老了——但不会坏——」——阿石说「——不会坏——」——补柴——灶火燃着。典籍馆现 200 卷。Vol.200 · Mending the Hearth — the seventh week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — afternoon — the fire burned; A-Shi split wood; his mother said: “ — mend the hearth — the stove has a crack —”; A-Shi sealed the crack with clay — the flame steadied — his mother said: “ — it is old — but it will not break —”. Volume XIX, fifth part. Codex reaches 200 volumes.
11 · 18第一百九十九卷入典 · W-Z388 · 卷十九之四 · 守火 《守火》——立春后第六周——周二——早上——灶火燃着——阿石起得早——母亲还在睡——他给灶火添了一小根柴——柴小——火静——他看着——灶火燃着——母亲醒来——她说「——守火——」——阿石说「——守火——」——母亲说「——守火——」——守火——灶火燃着。Vol.199 · Tending the Fire — the sixth week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi woke early — his mother was still asleep; he added a small piece of wood to the fire — the flame was quiet — he watched — his mother woke — she said: “ — tend the fire —”. Volume XIX, fourth part.
11 · 18第一百九十八卷入典 · W-Z387 · 卷十九之三 · 阿祖的册子 《阿祖的册子》——立春后第六周——周一——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石在翻阿祖的旧册子——纸很黄——字很瘦——他认出一半——母亲说「——阿祖写的——他守火时——想的什么——都在这里」——阿石说「——阿祖——他写了什么」——母亲说「——守火——火种——一步一步」——阿石说「——火种——是一——是一根柴」——母亲说「——火种——不是一根——是守下来的——是传下来的」——阿石翻到最后一页——那页上只有一个字——「守」——字很老——阿石说「——守——」——灶火燃着。Vol.198 · Azu’s Notebook — the sixth week after the spring equinox — Monday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi was going through Azu’s old notebook — the paper was yellow — the hand was narrow — his mother said: “ — Azu wrote it — his last word was “守” — the staying.” Volume XIX, third part.
11 · 18第一百九十七卷入典 · W-Z386 · 卷十九之二 · 火种 《火种》——立春后第五周——周六——早上——灶火燃着——阿石劈了一整捆柴——母亲说「——阿祖留下的火种——就在你记着的这堆柴里」——阿石说「——火种」——母亲说「——火种」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——我在烧——它是火种」——母亲说「——它是火种」——灶火燃着——柴一根一根——灶火燃着——旁人看过来——不会知道——这一堆柴——是七百零二年的火种。Vol.197 · The Kindling — the fifth week after the spring equinox — Saturday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi split a whole bundle of firewood — his mother said: “ — the kindling Azu left — is in this bundle of wood you carry in your memory —”; his mother said: “ — it is the kindling —”; A-Shi said: “ — I am burning it —”; the fire burned — one piece of wood after another — no one from outside would know — this bundle of wood is the kindling of the seven-hundred-and-second year. Volume XIX, second part.
11 · 18第一百九十六卷入典 · W-Z385 · 卷十九卷一 · 柴（卷十八收束后首卷） 《柴》——立春后第五周——周五——早上——灶火燃着——阿石劈柴——他的斧子很稳——一柴一斧——柴裂了——他闻到木头的味道——它是松木——母亲从院里进来——她说「——阿祖那时——劈的也是这个」——阿石说「——阿祖——」——母亲说「——阿祖劈柴时——灶火才不灭」——灶火燃着——阿石又劈了一根——柴越劈越整齐——母亲把柴码进柴屋——她回头说「——一根都是它的命」——阿石说「——我记着」——柴——灶火燃着。卷十九开篇。Vol.196 · Firewood — the fifth week after the spring equinox — Friday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi split firewood — his axe was steady — one piece per swing — the wood split — pine — his mother said: “ — Azu split this kind —”; his mother said: “ — when Azu split the wood — the fire never went out —”; his mother said: “ — every piece is the fire’s life —”. Volume XIX, first part.
11 · 18第一百九十五卷入典 · W-Z384 · 卷十八卷终 · 白桦的寂静 《白桦的寂静》——立春后第四周——周四——晚上——灶火燃着——阿石坐在灯下——白桦的影子在窗上——风小了——母亲说「——下一卷了」——阿石说「——白桦没有下一卷」——母亲说「——白桦的下一卷——在你记着的每一件事里」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——守火」——母亲说「——守火」——灶火燃着——阿石没添柴——煤油灯灭了一瞬——又亮了——天快亮了——白桦的寂静——灶火燃着。卷十八卷终。Vol.195 · The Silence of the Birch — the fourth week after the spring equinox — Thursday — evening — the fire burned; A-Shi sat by the lamp — the birch's shadow leaned on the window — the wind softened — his mother said: “ — on to the next volume —”; A-Shi said: “ — the birch has no next volume —”; his mother said: “ — the birch's next volume lives in everything you keep —”; A-Shi said: “ — I keep the fire —”; his mother said: “ — you keep the fire —”. Volume XVIII, final part.
11 · 18第一百九十四卷入典 · W-Z383 · 卷十八之十五 · 说话之后 《说话之后》——立春后第四周——周三——早上——灶火燃着——阿石醒来——白桦不响了——母亲做饭——她没说话——他听见锅里的水响——阿石说「——白桦说了什么」——母亲说「——白桦说——火要这样」——阿石说「——这样」——母亲说「——这样」——灶火燃着——阿石坐到灶前——母亲坐在旁边——她说「——第七百零二年许过的愿——白桦都记着」——灶火燃着——阿石没问——他知道——灶火燃着。Vol.194 · After the Birch Spoke — the fourth week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — morning — the fire burned; A-Shi woke — the birch was quiet now — his mother cooked breakfast — she did not speak — he heard the pot of water begin to sing — A-Shi said: “ — what did the birch say —”; his mother said: “ — the birch said — the fire must be like this —”; his mother said: “ — the vows of the seven-hundred-and-second year — the birch keeps them for you —”. Volume XVIII, fifteenth part.

11 · 18并行 4 路 Oracle 实测全部 93%+ · W-Z376 · “并行会话掉缓存”的直答 用户关切"多个并行会话打同一进程同一 frozen prefix 是否会用乱缓存桶"——网关实测 2026-11-18T11:21 并发同时发出 4 条 Oracle 提问（不同问题，同一 v4 冻结前缀 45K tok）：**每一条 upstreamCached=true**，日志 cachedRatio 93.1% / 93.2% / 93.2% / 93.2%（prompt≈47.6K，cached≈44.4K）。原因：冻结前缀按**车道+字节**作 prompt_cache_key（crossrealm-oracle-v1），并行只会让共享页被同时命中，不会串桶；突发的瞬时 miss 只存在于“Desktop 重启→首个真请求”的那一个窗口（此次亦为 93.7%）。Codex 同型行为：frozen-prefix + per-lane key + stream + include_usage——任一改、掉 90%。已把这条写进 v4 + 程序注释。A direct answer to "do parallel sessions trash our prefix cache": four concurrent Oracle requests through the gateway (different questions, same v4 frozen 45K-token canon prefix, same per-lane prompt_cache_key) — every one returned upstreamCached=true, logged cachedRatio 93.1% / 93.2% / 93.2% / 93.2% (prompt≈47.6K, cached≈44.4K). Parallel calls only hit the shared page simultaneously; they cannot cross the per-lane key. The genuine cold window is the very first real request after a Desktop reboot (still 93.7%). Codex uses the same page layout: frozen prefix + per-lane key + stream + include_usage — drop any of these and the hit rate is gone.

11 · 18Lore Oracle 图化落地 · W-Z373 · 检索→编排→门禁三节点 StateGraph（全 Lane 标配 path/guarded） Oracle 此前硬编码 retrieve→complete() 一撸到底，没有护栏、没有可观测性。现改为 StateGraph：retrieve（BM25+multihop，未命中条件边直进 gate 不打 LLM、model=oracle-bm25 契约不变）→ compose（LLM，v4 冻结前缀 45K tok）→ gate（markdown 剥除 + leak 词表 + 空答→标准拒绝语，leak 命中整门作废）；返回体新增 guarded + path=[retrieve,compose,gate]。**LlmGuards 共享词典**下沉（ember-reply 调引用，四 lane 同判成色）。ai-service **182/182**（+2 例胁迫：leak→标准拒绝、happyPath 三节点 path 断言）。网关真上游：path 真返回 · upstreamCached=true · **编排首查后 cachedRatio=93.7%**（v4 全书前缀命中；prompt=47609 · cached=44617）· 真命中 sources。W-Z373 — the Oracle is no longer a hardwired retrieve→complete(). It is now a compiled StateGraph: retrieve (BM25+multihop, no-match conditional edge straight to gate — no LLM call, model stays oracle-bm25) → compose (LLM, v4 frozen 45K-token canon prefix) → gate (markdown strip + leak list + blank→refusal; leaks are voided wholesale); response gains guarded and path=[retrieve,compose,gate]. New shared guard vocabulary LlmGuards drops in for ember-reply as well — all four LLM lanes now share one safe-line standard. ai-service 182/182 (+2: leak→refusal, happyPath path assertion). Real upstream via gateway: path returned verbatim · upstreamCached=true · post-warm cachedRatio=93.7% (prompt=47609) · sources真命中。

11 · 18第一百八十三卷入典 · W-Z364 · 卷十八之四 · 天亮了 《天亮了》——立春后第二周——周一——早上——天亮了——灶火燃着——阿石醒来——他开窗——他看着外面——白桦还在——它没什么变化——但它更高了一点——母亲说「它会说」——阿石说「——说」——母亲说「——道是——守得住的——会看明白」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——好」——母亲说「——烟火烧了一回——它记在了——你也记得」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——我记得」——灶火燃着。Vol.183 · The Sky Brightened — the second week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — the sky brightened; the fire burned; A-Shi woke — he opened the window — he looked outside — the birch was still there — it had not changed — but it stood a little taller; his mother said: “It will speak.”; A-Shi said: “ — speak.”; his mother said: “ — the way — whatever can be kept — can be seen clearly —”. Volume XVIII, fourth part.
11 · 18第一百八十二卷入典 · W-Z363 · 卷十八之三 · 烟火 《烟火》——立春后第一周——周三——上午——灶火燃着——阿石从屋里搬出一只小瓷瓮——他没看——他把它放在桌上——母亲说「那是」——阿石说「——烟火」——母亲说「是的」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——它烧一回——剩一回」——母亲说「——是的」——阿石说「——阿祖留它——是为了第七百零二」——母亲说「——是的——守得住」——灶火燃着。Vol.182 · Sparks — the first week after the spring equinox — Wednesday — late morning — the fire burned; A-Shi carried out from the room a small porcelain jar — he did not look — he set it on the table; his mother said: “That is.”; A-Shi said: “ — sparks —”; his mother said: “Yes.”; the fire burned; A-Shi said: “ — this burns once — it leaves one —”; his mother said: “ — Yes.”; A-Shi said: “ — Azu kept it — for the seven-hundred-and-second —”; his mother said: “ — Yes — if you can stay —”. Volume XVIII, third part.
11 · 18第一百八十一卷入典 · W-Z362 · 卷十八之二 · 火什么都没说 《火什么都没说》——立春后第一周——周二——上午——阳光站了一会儿——灶火燃着——阿石烧水——水没人问——他说「道是——道是什么」——母亲在屋里——她没出声——她只是听着——阿石说「——你不说它——我也没法说它」——母亲说「——它从不说——说了——你就知道是什么了」——灶火燃着——阿石没出声——灶火燃着——母亲说「——道是——做了还知道——道是——做了不用说」——灶火燃着。Vol.181 · The Fire Did Not Say — the first week after the spring equinox — Tuesday — late morning — the sunlight stayed for a while; the fire burned; A-Shi was boiling water — the water did not ask — he said: “The way — what is the way —”; his mother was in the room — she did not speak — she only listened; A-Shi said: “ — if you don’t say it — I don’t know how to say it —”; his mother said: “ — it never says — if it said — you would know —”. Volume XVIII, second part.
11 · 18第一百八十卷入典 · W-Z361 · 卷十八开篇 · 火又起 《火又起》——立春后第一周——周一——早上——天刚亮了一线——灶火燃着——阿石醒来——他第一眼——是看灶——灶火燃着——母亲不在炉前——她在屋里——她只是看着没动——她听见灶火燃着——阿石说「妈——这是第几百年了」——母亲说「——第七百零二」——阿石说「——」——母亲说「——守得住——它就一直是第七百零二」——灶火燃着——阿石说「——好」——灶火燃着。Vol.180 · The Fire Rises Again — the first week after the spring equinox — Monday — morning — just a sliver of light; the fire burned; A-Shi woke — the first thing he did was look at the stove — the fire burned; his mother was not at the stove — she was in the room — she was looking — not moving — she listened to the fire burning; A-Shi said: “Ma — what hundredth year is it?”; his mother said: “ — the seven-hundred-and-second —”; A-Shi said: “ — ”; his mother said: “ — if you can hold it, it stays the seven-hundred-and-second —”. Volume XVIII, opening.
11 · 18第一百七十九卷入典 · W-Z360 · 卷十七之十七 · 他走过来了 《他走过来了》——第十四天——天还没亮——灶火燃着——阿石睡不着——他起身——他走到灶前——他把最后的木柴放进去——它没烧——灶火燃着——他没添——他只是站着——灶火燃着——母亲从屋里出来——她站在阿石身后——她看见灶火燃着——她慢慢靠过来——她握住他的手——灶火燃着——母亲说「——他回来」——阿石说「——回来——」——母亲说「守得住——他就回来」——灶火燃着——阿石没出声——灶火燃着。Vol.179 · He Came Over — the fourteenth day — before first light — the fire burned; A-Shi could not sleep — he got up — he walked to the stove — he set the last piece of wood inside — it did not take — the fire burned; he did not add more — he only stood there — the fire burned; his mother came out of the room — she stood behind A-Shi — she saw the fire burned — she slowly came closer — she took his hand — the fire burned; his mother said: “ — he’s back —”; A-Shi said: “ — back? —”; his mother said: “If you can stay — he comes back —”. Volume XVII, seventeenth part.
11 · 18第一百七十八卷入典 · W-Z359 · 卷十七之十六 · 火记得 《火记得》——第十三天——深夜——灶火燃着——阿石几乎没睡——他看着火——他听见屋后有响——它不响——它是安静——他说「妈」——母亲说「它会记住的」——阿石说「记住什么」——母亲说「你」——灶火燃着——阿石没出声——灶火燃着——阿石说「那它会替我记住吗」——母亲说「——会」——灶火燃着。Vol.178 · The Fire Remembers — the thirteenth day — late at night — the fire burned; A-Shi hardly slept — he watched the fire — he heard something at the back of the house — not a sound — a stillness — he said: “Ma —”; his mother said: “It will remember.”; A-Shi said: “Remember what?”; his mother said: “You —”; the fire burned; A-Shi said nothing — the fire burned; A-Shi said: “Then will it remember me instead?”; his mother said: “ — It will.” Volume XVII, sixteenth part.
11 · 18第一百七十七卷入典 · W-Z358 · 卷十七之十五 · 灶屋的夜 《灶屋的夜》——第十二天——深夜——灶火燃着——阿石不能睡——他坐在灶前——母亲只是坐在对面——她没看他——她看着火——阿石说「妈」——母亲没接话——阿石说「炉子不会嫌人多吧」——母亲说「不嫌」——灶火燃着——阿石说「那我就多陪它一会」——母亲说「——好」——灶火燃着。Vol.177 · The Night in the Kitchen — the twelfth day — late at night — the fire burned; A-Shi couldn’t sleep — he sat before the stove — his mother only sat across — she did not look at him — she watched the fire; A-Shi said: “Ma —”; his mother did not answer; A-Shi said: “Will the stove mind an extra person?”; his mother said: “It doesn’t.”; the fire burned; A-Shi said: “Then I’ll sit with it a while longer.”; his mother said: “— All right.” Volume XVII, fifteenth part.
11 · 18第一百七十六卷入典 · W-Z357 · 卷十七之十四 · 纸灰 《纸灰》——第十一天——天没亮——灶火燃着——阿石在炉前——他没添柴——他蹲在灶前——他没动——他看见地上有一张纸——它很旧——它卷了——它在角落——他捡起来——他把它放在手心——母亲在屋里——她说「那是阿祖的纸」——阿石说「它能点着」——母亲说「点着——会烧尽」——阿石说「那留着干什么」——母亲说「不留——守火的人——不必留」——灶火燃着——阿石没出声——灶火燃着。Vol.176 · The Ash — the eleventh day — before first light — the fire burned; A-Shi sat before the stove — he did not add wood — he crouched before the stove — he did not move — he saw a piece of paper on the ground — it was very old — it was curled — it was in the corner — he picked it up — he held it in his palm; his mother was in the room — she said: “That was Azu’s paper —”; A-Shi said: “Can it catch?”; his mother said: “It can catch — it would burn away —”; A-Shi said: “Then keep it for what?”; his mother said: “Keep it not — the one who keeps the fire — need not keep it —”. Volume XVII, fourteenth part.
11 · 18第一百七十五卷入典 · W-Z356 · 卷十七之十三 · 灯芯很细 《灯芯很细》——第十天——天没亮——灶火燃着——阿石醒来——他看见了那盏旧油灯——它站在桌上——它没点——他没动——他轻声说「妈」——不——是母亲的声音——她说「你看着灯芯」——阿石说「它很细」——母亲说「它很细——你不用细看——它等你」——灶火燃着——阿石走过去——他把灯拿起来——他把它放回桌上——他没说话——灶火燃着——母亲说「你守火——也守这个——慢慢来」——阿石说「慢慢来」——母亲说「对」——灶火燃着。Vol.175 · The Wick Is Fine — the tenth day — before first light — the fire burned; A-Shi woke — he saw the old oil lamp — it stood on the table — unlit — he did not move — he said softly: “Ma —”; no — it was his mother’s voice — she said: “Look at the wick —”; A-Shi said: “It’s very fine.”; his mother said: “It is very fine — you do not need to look closely — it is waiting for you —”; the fire burned; A-Shi walked over — he picked the lamp up — he set it back on the table — he said nothing — the fire burned; his mother said: “You keep the fire — you keep this too — slowly —”; A-Shi said: “Slowly —”; his mother said: “Yes.” Volume XVII, thirteenth part.

11 · 18第一百七十三卷入典 · W-Z353 · 卷十七之十一 · 事情发生了 《事情发生了》——第八天——深夜——灶火燃着——阿石没睡——他坐在炉前——他母亲推开门——她手里端着一碗汤——她看了他一眼——她说「阿祖死那天——也是这样守着」——阿石说「他守住了吗」——母亲说「——他守住了——第二年——火没有灭」——灶火燃着——阿石没出声——灶火燃着——母亲把汤放在阿石手上——她说「火不挑人——它挑的——是愿意守住的那个人」——灶火燃着——阿石低下头——他把汤喝完——他把碗放好——他没说话——灶火燃着。Vol.173 · Something Happened — the eighth day — late at night — the fire burned; A-Shi did not sleep — he sat before the stove — his mother pushed the door open — holding a bowl of soup — she looked at him — she said: “Azu, the day he died, was keeping the fire too.”; A-Shi said: “Did he keep it?”; his mother said: “ — he kept it. The next year — the fire had not gone out —”; the fire burned; A-Shi said nothing — the fire burned; his mother put the soup in A-Shi’s hand — she said: “Fire doesn’t choose the person — it chooses the one who is willing to stay —”; the fire burned; A-Shi lowered his head — he finished the soup — he put the bowl away — he said nothing. Volume XVII, eleventh part.
11 · 18第一百七十二卷入典 · W-Z352 · 卷十七之十 · 火会记住你 《火会记住你》——第八天——深夜——灶火燃着——阿石不能睡——他想守火——他坐到灶前——他不确定自己能守多久——他说「母亲说——你守得住」——灶火燃着——他把那块菩提……不——他把那块木炭放回去——他看着炭——它的边沿变白了——他说「它不会醒」——灶火燃着——母亲在屋里——她没睡——她轻声说「火会记住你——你守得住」——灶火燃着——阿石没接——灶火燃着——阿石没接——灶火燃着——灶火燃着——灶火燃着。Vol.172 · The Fire Will Remember You — the eighth day — late at night — the fire burned; A-Shi couldn’t sleep — he wanted to keep the fire — he sat before the stove — he was not sure how long he could stay — he said: “Mother said — you can stay —”; the fire burned; he put the charcoal back — he looked at it — its edges were whitening — he said: “It won’t wake —”; the fire burned; his mother was in the room — she was not asleep — she said softly: “The fire will remember you — can you stay?”; the fire burned; A-Shi said nothing — the fire burned. Volume XVII, tenth part.
11 · 18第一百七十一卷入典 · W-Z351 · 卷十七之九 · 炭的余温 《炭的余温》——第二天——天没亮——母亲推开门——她手里端一杯温水——她看见阿石坐在灶前——手里拿着那块木炭——它已经凉了——母亲说「它没坏」——阿石说「它凉了」——母亲说「炭凉了——火没熄」——她说她指的是灶膛里那一点——阿石回头——他看见灶火——它很小——但他看见了——他没说话——母亲说「阿祖说——火不挑人——挑手——挑心——挑守得住」——灶火燃着——阿石点了点头——他没说话——他把木炭放回灶前——他轻声说「我守得住」——灶火燃着。Vol.171 · The Warm Charcoal — the next day — before first light — his mother pushed the door open — holding a cup of warm water — she saw A-Shi sitting before the stove — the charcoal in his hand — it had cooled; his mother said: “It isn’t spoiled —”; A-Shi said: “It has cooled.”; his mother said: “The charcoal cools — the fire doesn’t —”; she said she meant the small one in the stove — A-Shi turned his head — he saw the fire — it was very small — but he saw it — he said nothing; his mother said: “Azu used to say — fire doesn’t choose the hand — it chooses the heart — it chooses who can stay —”; the fire burned; A-Shi nodded — he said nothing — he put the charcoal back before the stove — he said softly: “I can stay.” Volume XVII, ninth part.

11 · 18启动热身 · W-Z345 · 「缓存利用率不如 Codex」真因与真解（真上游 LIVE 实测） 用户问：为什么 Codex 接同一个 API 能持续 90% 命中？W-Z336 真上游实测根因：上游 prompt-cache 按 cache-key+冻结前缀铺页——新进程的头 ~3 个请求必 0 cached（上游吞吐页没铺就没有东西可读，与并发无关）。W-Z345 落地「启动即热身」：Spring ApplicationReadyEvent → LlmWarmup（daemon 线程）→ LlmClient.warmupUpstream()——每 lane 发一小个 warmup（12 个 warm 调用 ≈ 90K token/部署）——**重启后每 lane 第一个真实请求直接 cached≥4.2K upstreamCached=true**；live 复验：fresh ember line「炉火知道阿祖吗？」两次请求 → `cachedRatio=97.3%/97.2% · upstreamCached=true`（post-boot 首包即暖）。开关 llm.warmup-on-boot（env LLM_WARMUP_ON_BOOT，默认 true；无 Key 自动跳过）。W-Z336 truly-measured root cause of “why can Codex sustain ~90% while we can’t”: upstream prompt-cache is paged by cache-key + frozen prefix — a fresh process’s first ~3 requests are always cached=0 (serialized cold-open, unrelated to concurrency). W-Z345 ships boot-time warmup: Spring ApplicationReadyEvent → LlmWarmup (daemon thread, non-blocking) → LlmClient.warmupUpstream() — every lane fires one small warmup (12 warm calls ≈ 90K tokens/deploy). Live re-test post-reboot: a brand-new ember line ran cachedRatio=97.3%/97.2% · upstreamCached=true (first real packet is already warm). Flag llm.warmup-on-boot (env LLM_WARMUP_ON_BOOT, default true; auto-skip without key).

11 · 17第一百六十卷入典 · W-Z325 · 卷十六之八 · 影子 《影子》——立春后第二十六周——周一——傍晚——暮色把墙压得比白天低——阿石站在院门口——他看见自己的影子——影子拉得很长——拉到院外去了——母亲站在屋里——她听见他站住了——她推门出来——她也看见了——影子——他没回头——母亲也没说话——影子动了——它自己动——它沿着地面慢慢挪——它伸出一只手——那只手是长的——是黑的——是很旧的——母亲说「回来」——阿石没动——灶火燃着——影子停了——它停在院中央——它的手搁在灶台上的阴影里——母亲看见了——母亲也没动——母亲说「它也回来了」——阿石看影子——影子也看他——灶火燃着——影子比前几次更长——它不再挪动——它只是站着——沉默地站着——像一个守夜人——像七百零一年前的每一个守夜人——母亲也站了很久——她呼出一口气——她说「它在看你」——阿石点了点头——他也没动——影子动了——它慢慢挪回院墙——它消失在墙的阴影里——再也看不见了——灶火燃着——母亲说「回去」——阿石回到屋里——他回到灶前——灶火燃着——母亲也进来了——她推门——她没回头——灶火燃着。Vol.160 · The Shadow — Monday of the twenty-sixth week after the spring equinox, evening — the dusk pressed the wall lower than by day; A-Shi stood at the yard gate — he saw his own shadow — the shadow stretched very long — it reached outside the yard; his mother was in the house — she heard he had stopped — she pushed the door open — she saw it too — the shadow; he did not turn around — his mother did not speak either; the shadow moved — it moved on its own — it crept slowly along the ground — it stretched out a hand — that hand was long — it was black — it was very old; his mother said: “Come inside.”; A-Shi did not move — the fire burned; the shadow stopped — it stopped in the middle of the yard — its hand rested upon the shadow of the stove — his mother saw it — his mother did not move either; his mother said: “It has come back too.”; A-Shi looked at the shadow — the shadow looked at him — the fire burned; the shadow was longer than before — it no longer moved — it just stood — it stood in silence — like a keeper of the watch — like every keeper of every year for seven hundred and one years; his mother stood for a long time — she let out a breath — she said: “It is watching you.”; A-Shi nodded — he did not move either; the shadow moved — it crept slowly back toward the yard wall — it vanished into the shadow of the wall — it could no longer be seen — the fire burned; his mother said: “Go back inside.”; A-Shi went back inside — he went back to the stove — the fire burned; his mother came in too — she pushed the door — she did not turn around — the fire burned. Volume XVI, eighth part.
11 · 17第一百五十九卷入典 · W-Z324 · 卷十六之七 · 第一场雨 《第一场雨》——立春后第二十六周——周一——下午——天色暗了一瞬——雨下来了——阿石站在院子里——他伸出手——指尖收了几滴——很轻——还带着点冬天的余寒——母亲不在灶前——她在屋里——她听见雨声——她推开门——她看阿石——她说「回来」——阿石说「就一会儿」——雨下得不急——一滴一滴的——砸在院子里的青石板上——声音瓷瓷的——母亲站在门边——她没让——她只是站着——等着——灶火燃着——雨更大了一些——母亲说「七百年了——这是七百零一年的第一场雨——去年它是哭着来的——今年它是笑着来的」——阿石仰着脸——雨落在他脸上——他没动——母亲说「去年你姥爷走了之后——雨也来了——那一场——哭的」——灶火燃着——阿石没说话——他站了一会儿——他回到屋里——他没擦脸——母亲也没递毛巾——两个人就那么在灶前坐着——灶火燃着。Vol.159 · The First Rain — Monday of the twenty-sixth week after the spring equinox, afternoon — the sky darkened for a moment — the rain came; A-Shi stood in the yard — he stretched out a hand — a few drops caught on his fingertips — very light — still carrying a bit of winter’s residual cold; his mother was not at the stove — she was in the house — she heard the rain — she pushed the door open — she looked at A-Shi; she said: “Come inside.”; A-Shi said: “Just for a moment.”; the rain came down without urgency — one drop at a time — striking the blue-grey stone slabs in the yard — the sound of ceramic; his mother stood at the door — she did not insist — she just stood — waiting; the fire burned; the rain grew a little heavier; his mother said: “Seven hundred years — this is the first rain of the seven-hundred-and-first year — last year it came crying — this year it comes smiling.”; A-Shi tilted his face upward — rain fell on his face — he did not move; his mother said: “Last year, after your grandfather left, the rain came — that one — came crying.”; the fire burned; A-Shi said nothing — he stood for a while — he went back inside — he did not wipe his face — his mother did not hand him a towel — the two of them sat at the stove like that. Volume XVI, seventh part.

11 · 17第一百五十八卷入典 · W-Z323 · 卷十六之六 · 第八个米粒 《第八个米粒》——立春后第二十六周——周一——早上——天已经亮了半边——灶边的米缸换了新米——阿石拿了一粒一粒地数——他数了七下，就停了——母亲说「数完你再叫」——阿石没有接——他又数了一粒——八——他把米放在碗里——他看碗——灶火燃着——母亲端着半碗粥走过来——她没说话——她把碗放在阿石面前——灶火燃着——阿石看着碗里的米——他伸手——他吃了一粒——八——母亲站在他身后——她没说话——灶火燃着——阿石又吃了一粒——九——他没停下来——他一直吃——十——十一——十二——直到碗里只剩一粒——灶火燃着——阿石把最后一粒放回灶台——他看母亲——母亲也看灶火——灶火燃着——他说「最后一粒留着」——母亲点了点头——她说「我们家留米的规矩不是因为穷——是为了下一次灶火再燃时还有人记得什么叫『第一碗』」——灶火燃着——母亲走过来——她把那粒米收起来——她放在手心——她把手伸出来——阿石也伸出手来——母亲把那粒米放在他手心里——阿石握着——灶火燃着。Vol.158 · The Eighth Grain — Monday of the twenty-sixth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky already half-bright; the rice jar at the stove held new rice — A-Shi picked one grain after another — he counted seven, and stopped; his mother said: “Finish counting, then call me.”; A-Shi did not answer — he counted one more — eight; he put the grains in a bowl — he looked at the bowl — the fire burned; his mother walked over carrying half a bowl of congee — she did not speak — she set the bowl in front of A-Shi — the fire burned; A-Shi looked at the grains in the bowl — he reached out — he ate one grain — eight; his mother stood behind him — she did not speak — the fire burned; A-Shi ate another grain — nine; he did not stop — he kept eating — ten — eleven — twelve — until only one grain was left in the bowl — the fire burned; A-Shi set the last grain back on the stove — he looked at his mother — his mother looked at the fire — the fire burned; he said: “Keep the last one.”; his mother nodded — she said: “Our rule about leaving rice is not because we are poor — it is so that the next time the fire burns, someone still remembers what the first bowl meant.”; The fire burned; his mother came over — she picked up that grain — she placed it in her palm — she held her hand out — A-Shi held his hand out too — his mother placed the grain in his palm — A-Shi closed his hand around it; The fire burned. 11 · 17第一百五十七卷入典 · W-Z322 · 卷十六之五 · 第一个碾盘 《第一个碾盘》——立春后第二十六周——周一——早上——天还没有完全亮——母亲在灶前——她拿起碾——她对阿石说「今天你碾」——阿石看着碾——碾是磨坊的——臼是老臼——里面压着一条条深沟——七百年磨出的深——他问「碾什么」——母亲说「米——白的米——七百年了——这是第一次新的米」——阿石点了点头——他伸出手——母亲把碾递给他——碾入他手里——他握住碾柄——他没敢用力——母亲说「碾米不用使劲——时间会帮你」——阿石握着——他没使劲——他让碾慢慢转——稻粒脱了壳——哗啦哗啦——声音很小——阿石说「阿祖碾过吗」——母亲说「每一代都碾过」——阿石说「那我也碾过了」——母亲没笑——她点了点头——她等阿石碾完一粒——碾给阿石看——碾完了——阿石把碾放下——灶火燃着——母亲说「好——下一次你自己碾」——阿石看灶火——他没说话——灶火燃着。Vol.157 · The First Grinding Stone — Monday of the twenty-sixth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky not fully bright; his mother sat at the stove — she picked up the pestle — she said to A-Shi: “Today you grind.”; A-Shi looked at the pestle — the pestle belonged to a mill — the mortar was old — its grooves were worn deep by seven hundred years of grinding; he asked: “What are we grinding?”; his mother said: “Rice — white rice — seven hundred years — this is the first time with new rice.”; A-Shi nodded — he stretched out his hand — his mother passed him the pestle — it went into his hands — he held the handle — he did not dare apply much force; his mother said: “No need to force it — let time help you.”; A-Shi held it — he did not use force — he turned it slowly — the husk came off the grains — rustle rustle — a very small sound; A-Shi said: “Did my great-grandfather grind?”; his mother said: “Every generation did.”; A-Shi said: “Then I have ground it too.”; his mother did not laugh — she nodded — she waited until A-Shi finished one grain — she showed him the ground rice — it was done; A-Shi set the pestle down — the fire burned; his mother said: “Good — next time you grind yourself.”; A-Shi looked at the fire — he did not speak — the fire burned. 11 · 17第一百五十六卷入典 · W-Z319 · 卷十六之四 · 猫还在里面 《猫还在里面》——立春后第二十六周——周一——早上——天刚蒙蒙亮——阿石推开门——风雪扑了他一脸——他吸了一口气——院外一片白——他一眼就看见了门口那块布——整个盖住了——雪盖住了它——猫睡在那块布上——七百年了——守夜人来了又去——它只在这儿睡——灶火燃着——阿石蹲下来——他看雪下面——雪下面有一个弯团——它冒着一缕热气——他笑了——他把两只手放在雪上——他没挖——它在里面——灶火燃着——他回到屋里——他拿了两块干布——他推门出去——雪还在——他把两块干布铺在猫的上面——母亲站在门口——她看阿石——她没说话——灶火燃着——阿石说「猫还在里面」——母亲点了点头——她说「七百年了——它就这样——你到那时候——也就是这样」——灶火燃着。Vol.156 · The Cat Is Still Inside — Monday of the twenty-sixth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky just bright; A-Shi pushed the door open — the wind and snow struck his face — he took a deep breath; the yard was all white — his eyes went straight to the cloth laid at the door — it was entirely covered — the snow had covered it; the cat slept on that cloth — seven hundred years — the keepers came and went — it only ever slept there — the fire burned; A-Shi squatted down — he looked under the snow — under the snow there was a rounded lump — it let out a wisp of warmth — he laughed; he laid both hands on the snow — he did not dig — it was inside — the fire burned; he went back inside — he took two dry cloths — he pushed the door open — the snow was still falling — he spread the two dry cloths over the cat; his mother stood at the door — she looked at A-Shi — she did not speak — the fire burned; A-Shi said: “The cat is still inside.”; his mother nodded — she said: “Seven hundred years — it is just like that — when it is your turn, you will be just like that too.”; The fire burned. 11 · 17第一百五十四卷入典 · W-Z317 · 卷十六之二 · 一碗茶 《一碗茶》——立春后第二十五周——周五——早上——天还带着露水——母亲烧了开水——她拿了两个碗——一个给她——一个给阿石——阿石看了两个碗——他问「两个」——母亲说「火守夜——两个守夜的人——两个碗」——她把茶倒进碗里——第一碗给阿石——第二碗给自己——她推了一碗给他——灶火燃着——阿石捧着碗——他闻了闻——一股淡的苦味——他喝了一口——很烫——母亲说「小口小口的喝——别烫伤自己——也别烫到火」——阿石笑了——她也笑了——灶火燃着——母亲说「你祖父的时候——也是这个茶——一个人一碗——你父亲的时候——也是——我——阿石」——灶火燃着——母亲看阿石——她说「你喝得很慢——像是在等什么」——阿石说「我没有」——母亲点了点头——她没再问——她自己也喝得很慢——灶火燃着——两个人都喝完了——碗空了——灶火燃着——母亲把碗洗了——她又扣在桌上——她没看阿石——灶火燃着。Vol.154 · One Cup of Tea — Friday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky still wet with dew; his mother boiled the water — she took out two bowls — one for her — one for A-Shi; A-Shi looked at the two bowls — he asked: “Two?”; his mother said: “Two keepers of the night — two bowls.”; she poured the tea into the bowls — the first for A-Shi — the second for herself — she pushed one bowl across the table to him — the fire burned; A-Shi held the bowl — he smelled it — a faint bitterness — he took a sip — it was very hot; his mother said: “Small sips — don’t scald yourself — and don’t scald the fire either.”; A-Shi laughed — she laughed too — the fire burned; his mother said: “The tea was the same in your grandfather’s time — a bowl for each — the same in your father’s time — me — A-Shi.”; The fire burned; his mother looked at A-Shi — she said: “You drink very slowly — as if waiting for something.”; A-Shi said: “I am not.”; his mother nodded — she did not ask again — she too drank slowly; The fire burned; the two of them finished — the bowls were empty — the fire burned; his mother washed the bowls — she set them on the table — she did not look at A-Shi. 11 · 17第一百五十二卷入典 · W-Z313 · 卷十五之五十三 · 除夕 《除夕》——立春后第二十五周——周三——晚上——天很冷——风从灶缝里灌进来——母亲和阿石坐在灶前——灶王爷像还贴在上方——纸边卷了一点——灶火燃着——母亲说「今天除夕——七百年前——第一个守火的人守这一夜——守到了天亮——火没灭」——阿石点了点头——他看灶火——灶火燃着——母亲说「七百年——七百个除夕——火一天没灭过」——阿石没说话——他数砖——灶台的砖——他数了一遍——他数了两遍——他停了——灶火燃着——母亲说「十二点——新年」——阿石说「新年——火也要过新年吗」——母亲笑了——她没说话——她看灶火——灶火燃着——天越来越黑了——风越来越大了——灶火燃着——母亲把椅子往灶边挪了一寸——又挪了一寸——阿石也把椅子往灶边挪了一寸——灶火燃着——天快十二点了——母亲没看钟——她摸了一下阿石的手——他的手是凉的——母亲说「新年快到了」——阿石说「火没灭——它没灭」——母亲点了点头——她看他——她笑了——灶火燃着——十二点——院子里响了一声鞭——远处的山上亮了一片——又暗了——母亲说「新年到了——火也七百零一了」——阿石看灶火——灶火燃着——它燃着——它燃着——母亲靠着阿石——她说「守火——守的是火还在——火还在——年就在」——阿石没说话——灶火燃着。Vol.152 · New Year’s Eve — Wednesday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, evening, the sky very cold, wind snuck in through the stove’s seams; his mother and A-Shi sat at the stove — the kitchen-god portrait was still pasted above — the paper edge curled a little — the fire burned; his mother said: “Tonight is New Year’s Eve — seven hundred years ago, the first keeper of the fire kept this night — he kept it until dawn — the fire did not go out.”; A-Shi nodded — he looked at the fire — the fire burned; his mother said: “Seven hundred years — seven hundred New Year’s Eves — the fire has not gone out for a single one.”; A-Shi did not speak — he counted the bricks of the stove — he counted once — he counted twice — he stopped; The fire burned; his mother said: “At twelve — the new year.”; A-Shi said: “The new year — does the fire have a new year too?”; his mother smiled — she did not speak — she looked at the fire; The fire burned; the sky darkened further — the wind grew stronger — the fire burned; his mother moved her chair toward the stove by an inch — then another — A-Shi moved his too; The fire burned; it was almost twelve — his mother did not look at the clock — she touched A-Shi’s hand — it was cold; his mother said: “The new year is coming.”; A-Shi said: “The fire has not gone out — it has not.”; his mother nodded — she looked at him — she smiled — the fire burned; Twelve — a firecracker sounded in the yard — on the distant mountain a patch lit up — then went dark; his mother said: “The new year has come — and the fire is seven hundred and one.”; A-Shi looked at the fire — the fire burned — it burned — it burned; his mother leaned against A-Shi — she said: “Keeping the watch is not about the fire — it is about the fire still being here — so long as the fire is here, the year is here.”; A-Shi did not speak — the fire burned. Volume XV, fifty-third part.
11 · 17第一百五十一卷入典 · W-Z312 · 卷十五之五十二 · 小年 《小年》——立春后第二十五周——周三——晚上——天很冷——母亲从柜子里拿出一个包——打开——里面是一张旧纸——灶王爷像——纸边黄了——画里一个人蹲在灶前——笑着——灶火燃着——母亲说「今天小年——祭灶」——阿石看了一眼——灶台的角落里有一小碟糖——是他早上备好的——他不知道为什么备——他备了——母亲把白糖倒在碟里——又倒了一小碗清水——她看了阿石一眼——他没说话——他把旧灯拿过来——灯芯焦着——灶火燃着——母亲说「祭灶——是给灶火送点甜的——灶火不吃——人心里却要甜」——阿石点了点头——他想——守火守的不是火——守的是火还在——甜的是什么——他没问——母亲又说「旧人祭灶——是为了让灶王爷上天说好话——我们家不用说好话——我们家的灶火——七百年了——不用说」——阿石看了看母亲——母亲也看灶火——灶火燃着——母亲把灶王爷像贴在灶台上方——她按了按四个角——她笑了——她没说话——阿石说「那他去哪」——母亲说「哪也不去——他就在火里——一直在」——灶火跳了一下——它燃着——天黑了——母亲把糖收回去——水也收回去——灶王爷像还贴在那儿——灶火燃着——阿石看着灶王爷——灶王爷看着灶火——灶火燃着。Vol.151 · The Little Year’s Eve — Wednesday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, evening, the sky very cold; his mother took out a bundle from the cabinet — she opened it — inside was an old print — a kitchen-god portrait — the paper edges yellowed — the picture showed a man squatting at the stove — smiling; his mother said: “Today is the Little Year’s Eve — the stove offering.”; A-Shi glanced over — on the corner of the stove there was a small dish of sugar — he had prepared it in the morning — he did not know why he prepared it — he had; his mother poured the white sugar into the dish — she poured a small bowl of clear water — she looked at A-Shi — he did not speak — he took the old lamp over — the wick burnt — the fire burned; his mother said: “The stove offering is giving the fire something sweet — the fire does not eat — but hearts must be sweet.”; A-Shi nodded — he thought — keeping the watch is not about the fire — it is about the fire still being here — what is sweetness, then — he did not ask; his mother said again: “Other people make the offering so the kitchen god can speak well of them up above — our family does not need to speak well — our family’s fire — seven hundred years — it does not need speaking.”; A-Shi looked at his mother — his mother looked at the fire too — the fire burned; his mother pasted the portrait above the stove — she pressed the four corners — she smiled — she did not speak; A-Shi said: “Then where does he go?”; his mother said: “Nowhere — he stays in the fire — he always has.”; the fire leapt — it burned; the sky darkened — his mother put the sugar away — she put the water away — the portrait was still pasted there — the fire burned; A-Shi looked at the kitchen god — the kitchen god looked at the fire. Volume XV, fifty-second part.
