11 · 17W-Z310 · 第150卷入典 · 剩下的半夜 · 守火守的是「火还在」 《剩下的半夜》——立春后第二十五周——周二——深夜——只剩灶火的光——阿石守了一夜——他没睡——天快亮的时候——他靠着灶台边睡着了——他不知道什么时候睡着的——他只记得灶火还在——灶火燃着——他醒来——天还是黑的——灶火还在——灶火燃着——母亲不在屋里——她不知道什么时候出去了——灶火燃着——阿石站起来——他看灶火——灶火没有灭——他又坐下——他又看灶火——灶火燃着——他数——一——二——三——他数到七——他停了——他继续数——一——二——三——他又数到七——他停了——灶火燃着——他想——守火不是不睡——守火是「不走」——他又看灶火——灶火燃着——他扶了扶旧灯的位置——灯芯焦着——没有油——灶火燃着——母亲回来了——她站在门口——她看了他一眼——她没说话——灶火燃着——母亲说「灶火没灭」——阿石说「灶火没灭」——母亲走过来——她坐下——她看灶火——她说「守火守的不是火——守的是火还在」——灶火燃着——母亲又说「你睡着了——它也没灭」——阿石没说话——灶火燃着——天一点一点亮了——灶火燃着。Vol.150 · The Rest of the Night — Tuesday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, deep night, only the firelight left; A-Shi kept the watch through the night — he did not sleep — toward dawn — he dozed off against the stove’s edge; he did not know when he had fallen asleep — he only remembered the fire was still there — the fire burned; he woke — it was still dark — the fire was still there — the fire burned; his mother was not in the room — he did not know when she had gone out — the fire burned; A-Shi stood up — he looked at the fire — it had not gone out — he sat again — he looked at the fire again; The fire burned; he counted — one — two — three — he counted to seven — he stopped — he counted again — one — two — three — he counted to seven — he stopped; The fire burned; he thought — keeping the watch is not about not sleeping — it is about not leaving; he looked at the fire again — the fire burned — he straightened the old lamp a little — the wick burnt — there was no oil — the fire burned; his mother came back — she stood at the doorway — she looked at him — she said nothing — the fire burned; his mother said: “The fire did not go out.”; A-Shi said: “The fire did not go out.”; his mother came over — she sat down — she watched the fire — she said: “Keeping the watch is not about the fire — it is about the fire still being here.”; The fire burned; his mother said again: “You fell asleep — and it did not go out.”; A-Shi did not speak — the fire burned; the sky brightened little by little — the fire burned. Volume XV, fifty-first part.
11 · 17第一百四十九卷入典 · W-Z309 · 卷十五之五十 · 第一夜 《第一夜》——立春后第二十五周——周二——晚上——天全黑了——母亲吃完饭——她站起来——她把围裙解下来——她放在桌上——她对阿石说「今夜——你自己守」——阿石抬头——他没说话——他看了灶火——灶火燃着——母亲走到门口——她停了——她没回头——「灶火灭了——你就走」——她出了门——院子里很安静——院门关了——门闩响了一声——阿石一个人站在灶前——灶火燃着——他坐下来——他看灶火——他数灶火跳动了几次——他数到七——他停了——他想起母亲说的话——「点一次——烧七百年」——他想起那行字——他把旧灯拿过来——他放在灶边——灯芯焦着——没有油——灶火燃着——阿石说「我在」——灶火跳了一下——它没灭——它燃着——夜很深了——他没睡——他就那么坐着——他看灶火——灶火燃着——他数着灶火——一——二——三——他数到七——他又从头数——一——二——三——天快亮了——灶火还是燃着——母亲回来了——她站在门口——她看阿石——她没说话——灶火燃着——母亲说「它没灭」——阿石说「它没灭」——母亲走过来——她坐下——她和阿石一起看灶火——灶火燃着。Vol.149 · The First Night — Tuesday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, evening, the sky fully dark; his mother finished supper — she stood up — she untied her apron — she laid it on the table; she said to A-Shi: “Tonight — you keep the watch alone.”; A-Shi lifted his head — he did not speak — he looked at the fire — the fire burned; his mother walked to the door — she stopped — she did not turn around — “If the fire goes out, you go too.” she said; she went out — the yard was very quiet — the gate closed — the latch sounded once; A-Shi stood alone at the stove — the fire burned; he sat down — he watched the fire — he counted how many times it jumped — he counted seven — he stopped; he thought of his mother’s words — “Light it once — burn for seven hundred years” — he thought of the line on the old lamp; he took the old lamp over — he set it by the stove — the wick burnt — there was no oil — the fire burned; A-Shi said: “I’m here.”; the fire leapt once — it did not go out — it burned; the night deepened — he did not sleep — he just sat — he watched the fire — the fire burned; he counted the fire — one — two — three — he counted seven — he started over — one — two — three; the sky was almost bright — the fire still burned; his mother came back — she stood at the door — she looked at A-Shi — she said nothing — the fire burned; his mother said: “It did not go out.”; A-Shi said: “It did not go out.”; his mother came over — she sat down — she watched the fire with him. Volume XV, fiftieth part.
11 · 17W-Z308 · LLM 探测链路复修 · Oracle 双轨复测 97.3% / 97.5% 「探测用的谎言机」被换了：自写 CONNECT+TLS 隧道在 Clash 7897 上稳定拿到「没有响应头」的失败，同一链路 curl 实测 HTTP 200 且 usage 完整——scripts/_curl-proxy.mjs 出口上线（LLM_PROXY_URL=http://127.0.0.1:7897）；cache-probe 复测 warm cached_tokens=5120/5344 ≈ 95.8%，Oracle 双轨 canon 97.3%（6784/6973）、refs 97.5%（7424/7611）。The broken ruler got replaced: the hand-rolled CONNECT+TLS tunnel reliably failed on Clash 7897 while plain curl returned HTTP 200 with a complete usage block on the same chain. New outlet `scripts/_curl-proxy.mjs` (LLM_PROXY_URL=http://127.0.0.1:7897); cache-probe re-measured warm cached_tokens=5120/5344 ≈ 95.8%; Oracle lanes re-measured canon 97.3% (6784/6973) and refs 97.5% (7424/7611).
11 · 17第一百四十八卷入典 · W-Z307 · 卷十五之四十九 · 上一盏灯 《上一盏灯》——立春后第二十五周——周二——早上——天刚亮——阿石醒来——灶火还燃着——母亲已经在灶前坐着——她没看他——灶台角落里有一盏灯——旧灯——石座——没有灯罩——灯芯是焦的——阿石从没见过——他走过去——母亲也没拦——她只是看灶火——阿石拿起旧灯——很轻——里面没有油——灯座上刻着一行字——他看不清楚——他把灯凑近灶火——灶火的光照着字——字很深——刻得很老——母亲说「你姥爷守夜的时候用的」——阿石没说话——他看着那行字——他慢慢看懂了——上面写着「点一次——烧七百年」——他抬头看母亲——母亲还是看灶火——她没抬头——母亲说「他走之前——把它放在灶前——他说——下一个守夜的人——是你」——阿石没动——灶火燃着——母亲说「它不是灯——它是一个东西——它记了七百年——它记了你阿祖——它记了你姥爷——它记了你父亲——它记了你——它还会记下去」——阿石把旧灯放回原处——他又看它——灯芯焦着——没有油——母亲说「不用油——灶火就是它的油——灶火不灭——它就在」——阿石看着灶火——灶火燃着——母亲站起来——她走到阿石旁边——她把手放在他的头上——她没说话——她只放着——灶火燃着。Vol.148 · The Previous Lamp — Tuesday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky just bright; A-Shi woke — the fire still burned — his mother was already sitting at the stove — she did not look at him; in the corner of the stove there was a lamp — an old lamp — a stone base — no shade — the wick burnt — A-Shi had never seen it; he walked over — his mother did not stop him — she only looked at the fire; A-Shi picked up the old lamp — it was very light — there was no oil in it — on the stone base there was a line of characters — he could not make them out — he held the lamp close to the stove — the firelight fell on the line — the characters were deep — they were carved long ago; his mother said: “Your mother’s father kept the night-watch with this.”; A-Shi did not speak — he looked at the line — he slowly made it out — it read: “Light it once — burn for seven hundred years.”; he lifted his head to his mother — his mother still looked at the fire — she did not look up; his mother said: “Before he left, he set it at the stove — he said — the next keeper of the watch — is you.”; A-Shi did not move — the fire burned; his mother said: “It is not a lamp — it is a thing — it remembers seven hundred years — it remembers your great-grandfather — it remembers your grandfather — it remembers your father — it remembers you — it will keep remembering.”; A-Shi set the old lamp back — he looked at it again — the wick burnt — there was no oil; his mother said: “No oil — the fire itself is its oil — so long as the fire burns, it is.”; A-Shi looked at the fire — the fire burned; his mother stood up — she came over and laid her hand on his head — she said nothing — she just kept it there. Volume XV, forty-ninth part.
11 · 17第一百四十七卷入典 · W-Z306 · 卷十五之四十八 · 说出了「火」字之后 《说出了「火」字之后》——立春后第二十五周——周一——晚上——天全黑了——阿石站在灶前——他没动——他也没说话——他只看灶火——母亲做了一个动作——她把自己的盘子推到阿石面前——她没说话——阿石没动盘子——他没抬头——母亲出去了——她回来了——她端着干净碗——她放在桌上——她什么都没说——阿石慢慢走过来——他看了母亲——他看灶火——灶火燃着——母亲看阿石——她走过来——她从后面轻轻拍了他的肩——他没动——灶火燃着——母亲说「它烧了七百年」——阿石没出声——灶火燃着——母亲说「它烧了七百年——它烧了你阿祖——它也烧了你姥爷——它也会烧你——它还会烧下去」——灶火燃着——阿石又说「我知道」——母亲点了点头——她没再说——灶火燃着——两个人都看灶火——灶火燃着——阿石慢慢蹲下来——他把脸贴着自己的膝盖——他没哭——他也没说话——母亲蹲到灶火前——灶火燃着——两个人就那么蹲着——灶火燃着。Vol.147 · After He Said the Word — Monday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, evening, the sky fully dark; A-Shi stood at the stove — he did not move — he did not speak — he only watched the fire; his mother made one motion — she pushed her own bowl across to him — she did not speak — A-Shi did not move the bowl — he did not lift his head; his mother went out — she came back — she carried clean bowls — she set them on the table — she said nothing; A-Shi slowly walked over — he looked at his mother — he looked at the fire — the fire burned; his mother looked at A-Shi — she came over — she softly patted his shoulder from behind — he did not move; The fire burned; his mother said: “It has burned seven hundred years.”; A-Shi did not answer — the fire burned; his mother said: “It burned seven hundred years — it burned your grandfather — and it burned your mother’s mother — and it will burn you too — it will keep burning.”; The fire burned; A-Shi said: “I know.”; his mother nodded — she said nothing more — the fire burned; The two of them looked at the fire — the fire burned; A-Shi slowly squatted down — he pressed his face into his knees — he did not cry — he did not speak; his mother squatted at the fire too — the fire burned; The two of them just squatted there — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-eighth part.
11 · 17第一百四十六卷入典 · W-Z305 · 卷十五之四十七 · 明天再说 《明天再说》——立春后第二十五周——周一——早上——天很灰——郭阿婆说了明天再说——今天她来了——她带了一件事——她把纸牌留在桌上——她要走——阿石叫住她——「你走」——郭阿婆停了——她没有回头——她没说话——阿石出去追——她已经走到院门口——院外天很亮——他追到她旁边——「今天不能说」——他问——郭阿婆终于回头——她看他——她没说话——他看了灶火方向——朝灶口去了——他转身往回走——郭阿婆也回去了——两个人一起回去——母亲站在灶前——灶火燃着——没人抬头——灶火燃着——阿石蹲下去——他握着自己的两只手——他没说话——母亲也没说话——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「都坐下」——母亲也坐下——阿石也坐下——郭阿婆说「今天就说」——她把纸牌摆在桌上——她没看灶火——她只看他们——「第七张——你已经记住了」——阿石没抬头——母亲也没动——灶火燃着——郭阿婆又说「你说吧」——阿石还是没抬头——他看自己的手——他攥紧——他又松开——他慢慢说了一个字——母亲看了他一眼——郭阿婆也看到了——灶火燃着——郭阿婆点了点头——「对了」——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「这个字叫火——它烧了七百年」——阿石没接——灶火燃着——郭阿婆把那几张纸牌收起来——她走了——她没说话——她出了门——灶火燃着——郭阿婆走了——阿石蹲在灶前——母亲坐在灶前——灶火燃着——母亲拍了拍他的肩——她没开口——灶火燃着。Vol.146 · Tomorrow, Then — Monday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky very grey; Aunt Guo had said “tomorrow” — she came today — she brought one thing — she set the cards on the table — she turned to leave; A-Shi stopped her — “You’re going?”; Aunt Guo stopped — she did not turn around — she said nothing; A-Shi chased her out — she was already at the gate — it was bright outside the gate — he caught up with her — “Can’t we say it today?” he asked; Aunt Guo finally turned around — she looked at him — she said nothing — he saw the stove in the direction of the kitchen — he walked back toward it; he turned around — Aunt Guo turned too — the two of them walked back together; his mother stood at the stove — the fire burned — no one looked up — the fire burned; A-Shi crouched — he held his two hands — he did not speak — his mother said nothing either; The fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “Everyone sit.” — his mother sat — A-Shi sat too; Aunt Guo said: “We say it today.” — she laid the cards on the table — she did not look at the fire — she only looked at the two of them — “The seventh card — you’ve already learned it.”; A-Shi did not lift his head — his mother did not move — the fire burned; Aunt Guo said again: “Say it.”; A-Shi still did not lift his head — he looked at his own hands — he closed them — he opened them again; he spoke one character slowly; his mother looked at him — Aunt Guo saw it too — the fire burned; Aunt Guo nodded — “That’s it.”; the fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “This character — fire — it burned for seven hundred years.”; A-Shi did not answer — the fire burned; Aunt Guo gathered the few cards — she left — she said nothing — she went through the door; the fire burned; Aunt Guo had gone — A-Shi crouched at the stove — his mother sat at the stove — the fire burned; his mother patted his shoulder — she did not speak — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-seventh part.
11 · 17第一百四十五卷入典 · W-Z304 · 卷十五之四十六 · 纸牌上第几个字 《纸牌上第几个字》——立春后第二十五周——周日——下午——天很亮——郭阿婆把纸牌一张一张摆在灶台上——一叠七张——她数到最上面——阿石看着——他没出声——母亲也蹲在灶前——灶火燃着——郭阿婆翻了一张——把字对着阿石——「记住了」——她说——阿石点了点头——他嘴唇动了——没出声——灶火燃着——郭阿婆把那一张折起来——她收回去——她又拿出一张——同样的字——她把这张递给阿石——他又看了很久——他把它叠了一下——他又摊开——他又看了——妈说「你袖子沾了」——阿石没动——母亲没说话——灶火燃着——阿石忽然笑了——他笑得很小——郭阿婆看他——她没接话——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「记住了——第七张」——阿石收好那几张——他放回灶台上——他看了母亲——母亲也在笑——灶火燃着——郭阿婆又说「明天再说」——阿石点了点头——灶火燃着——母亲也点了点头——郭阿婆也点了点头——灶火燃着。Vol.145 · The Number on the Card — Sunday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, afternoon, the light crisp and bright; Aunt Guo laid the cards out one by one on the stove — a stack of seven — she counted to the top; A-Shi watched — he did not speak — his mother sat at the stove too — the fire burned; Aunt Guo turned one over — she held the character up to A-Shi — “Remembered it?” she said; A-Shi nodded — his lips moved — he did not speak — the fire burned; Aunt Guo folded that one — she took it back — she drew another out — the same character — she handed this one to A-Shi; he looked for a long time — he folded it once — he unfolded it — he looked again; his mother said: “Your sleeve is stained.”; A-Shi did not move; his mother did not speak — the fire burned; A-Shi suddenly smiled — it was a small smile — Aunt Guo watched him — she did not answer — the fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “Remembered. The seventh card.”; A-Shi put the cards away — he set them back on the stove — he looked at his mother — she was smiling too; The fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “We’ll talk tomorrow.”; A-Shi nodded — the fire burned; his mother nodded — Aunt Guo nodded too — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-sixth part.
11 · 17第一百四十四卷入典 · W-Z303 · 卷十五之四十五 · 炉火静默的第七天 《炉火静默的第七天》——立春后第二十五周——周六——午后——天很静——整整一周——阿石又数了三张——第六张——是白字——他刚认了——那个字是「休」——他说了出来——灶火燃着——母亲坐在灶前——她蹲着——她手边没有茶——她只看灶火——郭阿婆出去了——她去门外——阿石没动——灶火燃着——母亲没问她去了哪儿——灶火燃着——阿石把那张纸牌又拿起来——他看了——他看了很久——母亲看他——「你记得住吗」——母亲问——「记住了」——阿石说——母亲没接——她看灶火——灶火燃着——傍晚——郭阿婆回来了——她进来——灶火燃着——她只说「该教下一张了」——阿石点了点头——母亲没说——灶火燃着——郭阿婆把纸牌摆在桌上——她一张一张数——数到第七张——灶火燃着。Vol.144 · The Seventh Silent Day — Saturday of the twenty-fifth week after the spring equinox, afternoon, the sky very quiet; A full week had passed — A-Shi had already learned the sixth card; the sixth card — the white character — was “休” — he said it aloud — the fire burned; his mother sat at the stove — she was crouching too — there was no tea at her side — she only watched the fire; Aunt Guo went out — she went beyond the door — A-Shi did not move — the fire burned; his mother did not ask where she had gone — the fire burned; A-Shi picked up that card again — he looked at it — he looked for a long time; his mother looked at him — “Can you keep it?” she asked; “I can,” A-Shi said; his mother did not reply — she watched the fire — the fire burned; Toward evening — Aunt Guo came back — she came in — the fire burned — she said only: “It’s time for the next one.”; A-Shi nodded — his mother said nothing — the fire burned; Aunt Guo spread the cards on the table — she counted them one by one — she counted up to the seventh — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-fifth part.
11 · 17第一百四十三卷入典 · W-Z302 · 卷十五之四十四 · 纸牌上的那个字 《纸牌上的那个字》——立春后第二十三周——周日——上午——天色很白——阿石还没说话——郭阿婆还没开口——母亲也没动——灶火燃着——阿石捧着自己那碗稀饭——他又看了那张纸牌——他已经记住了那个字——妈说「好了——吃了」——他喝完了——他放下碗——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「记住了」——阿石点了点头——他没张嘴——灶火燃着——母亲出去拿了个簸箕——簸的莲子——她放灶台上——她没抬头——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「吃饱了——给你看第二张」——阿石停了——他抬头——灶火燃着——郭阿婆从包里拿出来一叠牌——她数——数了十三张——她用拇指翻了一张——字不同——她把它翻过来——这个字阿石刚认过——阿石一眼就看见了——妈没说话——灶火燃着——阿石说「两个字——一个在灶火上——一个在灶火下」——郭阿婆没接——她没抬头——她收牌——她放好——她说「自己蹲」——灶火燃着——阿石蹲灶前——灶火照着他——他数灶火——他没出声——母亲看着灶火——郭阿婆也看灶火——灶火燃着。Vol.143 · The Word on the Card — Sunday of the twenty-third week after the spring equinox, morning, the sky very white; A-Shi had not spoken — Aunt Guo had not opened her mouth — his mother had not moved either; the fire burned; A-Shi held his bowl of thin porridge — he looked at the card again — he had remembered the character; his mother said: “Done — eat now”; he finished — he put the bowl down — the fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “Remembered it?”; A-Shi nodded — he did not open his mouth — the fire burned; his mother went out — she took a sieve — she came back holding lotus seeds — she set them on the stove — she did not look up; the fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “Now that you’re fed — I’ll show you the second one.”; A-Shi stopped — he lifted his head — the fire burned; Aunt Guo drew out a stack of cards — she counted — she counted thirteen — she turned one over with her thumb — the character was different; she turned it over — this was the same character A-Shi had just learned — A-Shi saw it at a glance; her mother said nothing — the fire burned; A-Shi said: “Two characters — one above the fire — one beneath.”; Aunt Guo did not answer — she did not look up — she gathered the cards — she put them away — she said: “Squat and look.”; A-Shi squatted in front of the stove — the fire lit him — he counted the fire — he said nothing — his mother watched the fire — Aunt Guo watched the fire too — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-fourth part.
11 · 17第一百四十二卷入典 · W-Z301 · 卷十五之四十三 · 写上去的那张纸牌 《写上去的那张纸牌》——立春后第二十三周——周日——早上——阳光刚出来——郭阿婆第三天又来了——她带着一只小笔——和一张空纸牌——她说「阿石——你看」——阿石转过头——他看见郭阿婆手里握着——她把那张空纸牌放在桌上——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「阿祖生前——说你能认一张」——阿石没问——灶火燃着——郭阿婆拿一枝小笔——她沾了点墨——她在纸牌上写了一个字——她没抬头——她写完了——她把纸牌递给阿石——阿石接住——他看那一张——那一个字——是这个家灶火说不出的字——阿石认得——他没说话——阿石看了它很久——他没动——灶火燃着——母亲来了——她看见桌上那张纸牌——她停了一下——她什么都没说——郭阿婆把它翻过去——背面是一个日期——「七百年，三个月，十二天」——母亲看见了——她没说话——阿石手心发热——他没低头——灶火燃着——阿石说「好了」——他把牌放回去——母亲点了点头——灶火燃着——郭阿婆把牌收起来——她说「以后我天天再教你一张」——阿石点了点头——母亲也点了点头——灶火燃着。Vol.142 · The Card She Wrote On — Sunday of the twenty-third week after the spring equinox, morning, the sun had just come up; Aunt Guo came a third day — she carried a small brush — and a blank card; she said: “A-Shi — look.”; A-Shi turned his head — he saw what Aunt Guo held — she set the blank card on the table; the fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “Before the old man died — he said you would be able to read one.”; A-Shi did not ask — the fire burned; Aunt Guo took a small brush — she dipped it in ink — she wrote one character on the card — she did not lift her head — she finished writing; she handed the card to A-Shi — he took it — he looked at that one; that one character — the one this house’s fire could not say — A-Shi knew it — he said nothing; A-Shi looked at it for a long time — he did not move — the fire burned; his mother came — she saw the card on the table — she paused a step — she said nothing — she did not leave either; Aunt Guo turned the card over — on the back was a date — “seven hundred years, three months, twelve days” — Mother saw it — she said nothing; A-Shi’s palm was warm — he did not lower his head — the fire burned; A-Shi said: “Done.” — he set the card back; his mother nodded — the fire burned; Aunt Guo gathered the cards — she said: “From now on I’ll teach you one every day.”; A-Shi nodded — his mother nodded too — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-third part.
11 · 17第一百四十一卷入典 · W-Z300 · 卷十五之四十二 · 灶口的纸牌 《灶口的纸牌》——立春后第二十三周——周六——午后——天很薄——母亲又把白瓷碗放回灶台——她没说什么——郭阿婆今天又来了——她背着一个包——包里一样东西——她没说是什么——郭阿婆放下包——她从里面拿出一叠纸牌——灶火没动——阿石没问——他继续扫笤帚——他扫了很久——「洗洗手」——郭阿婆说——阿石停了——他把手放在水盆里——他抬头看郭阿婆——郭阿婆点了点头——郭阿婆把那副牌一张一张摆在桌上——灶火没动——母亲回来了——她拎着米——她看见了——她停了一步——她什么都没说——阿石看见了——他继续扫——他扫了很久——母亲的米放在台面上——母亲回到灶前——她又添了一根白桦——灶火燃着——母亲说「纸牌什么时候教的」——郭阿婆没抬头——「阿祖给我的」——她说——没人接——灶火燃着——郭阿婆教阿石认牌——一张一张翻——他一张一张看——他没出声——灶火照着他的手——他看完了——他说「好了」——母亲端来碗——米粥——四个人都坐下了——没人说话——灶火燃着——郭阿婆说「别急——以后天天教你一张」——阿石点了点头——灶火燃着。Vol.141 · The Card by the Stove — Saturday of the twenty-third week after the spring equinox, afternoon, the sky very thin; Mother set the white porcelain bowl back on the stove-top — she said nothing; Aunt Guo came again — she carried a bag — one thing in it — she did not say what; Aunt Guo set the bag down — she drew out a stack of paper cards — the fire did not move; A-Shi did not ask — he kept the broom — he swept once — he swept again; “Wash your hands,” Aunt Guo said; A-Shi stopped — he dipped his hands in the basin — he looked up at Aunt Guo — Aunt Guo nodded; Aunt Guo laid the cards out one by one on the table — the fire did not move; his mother came back — she carried the rice — she saw them — she paused a step — she said nothing; A-Shi saw — he kept sweeping — he swept for a long time; Mother put the rice on the counter — Mother came back to the stove — she fed one more white-birch branch — the fire burned; Mother said: “When did you learn those cards?”; Aunt Guo did not look up — “The old man gave them to me,” she said; no one answered — the fire burned; Aunt Guo taught A-Shi the cards — one by one — he studied each — he said nothing — the fire lit his hands — he finished — he said “Done.”; Mother brought the bowls — rice gruel — the four of them sat — no one spoke — the fire burned; Aunt Guo said: “Don’t rush — I’ll teach you one a day.”; A-Shi nodded — the fire burned. Volume XV, forty-second part.
11 · 17第一百四十卷入典 · W-Z299 · 卷十五之四十一 · 炉火静了七天 《炉火静了七天》——立春后第二十二周——周六——午后——天很平——这一周——郭阿婆没来——灶火依旧燃着——母亲照样添白桦——阿石也照样守——他们没说话——母亲把白瓷碗从灶台移到碗架上——她拭过灶台——她扫过了一遍——她也没说什么——周叔第二天又来了——他站在灶前——灶火燃着——他没进门——母亲问他——他说不等什么——他说他去看鸡——他说得很轻——灶火燃着——第三天早上——天还没亮——阿石先去了灶房——他往灶膛里看——它燃得匀——他把白瓷碗搁在碗架上最上层——灶火照着它——它依旧很白——阿石把手伸得很近——他没碰碗——他感觉到了——手掌和碗之间那一层热——母亲进来了——她端了两碗稀饭——她没说话——阿石没开口——灶火燃着。Vol.140 · Seven Days of Silent Fire — Saturday of the twenty-second week after the spring equinox, afternoon, the sky very flat; this week — Aunt Guo did not come; the fire burned all the same — mother fed the birch — A-Shi kept watch — they did not speak of it either; Mother moved the white porcelain bowl from the stove to the shelf — she wiped down the stove — she swept it once — she said nothing; Uncle Zhou came the next day again — he stood at the stove — the fire burned — he did not come in; Mother asked him — he said nothing — he said it very softly; the fire burned; On the third day — A-Shi went straight to the stove-house — he looked into the firebox — it burned very evenly; he glanced at the shelf — the white bowl sat at the top — the fire lit it — it was very white — and whiter than the day before; he reached out very close to it — he did not touch it — he felt the heat against the back of his hand; He said nothing — his mother woke — she carried two bowls of porridge — she said nothing — she set them on the table — she did not touch the white bowl either; A-Shi picked up his bowl — he looked at the fire — he did not speak. Volume XV, forty-first part.
11 · 17第一百三十九卷入典 · W-Z298 · 卷十五之四十 · 粥不见了 《粥不见了》——立春后第二十一周——周六——午后——天起了风——阿石放学回来——他冲进灶前——全家人都坐在那——灶火燃着——桌子上——空了——母亲看著灶火——她没说话——阿石看了那只白瓷碗——它搁在桌角——他又看了灶火——母亲说「灶火替我们收了」——她没动——阿石没动——周叔没动——郭阿婆也没动——小火也没动——母亲把那只白瓷碗翻过来——底下是空的——郭阿婆说「它吃饱了——它开春了」——周叔清了清嗓子——他没出声——灶火燃着。Vol.139 · The Porridge Is Gone — Saturday of the twenty-first week after the spring equinox, afternoon, wind rose against the courtyard; A-Shi came home from outside — he rushed to the stove — the whole family was sitting there — the fire burned; On the table — it was empty; Mother was looking at the stove — she did not speak; A-Shi looked at the white porcelain bowl — it stood at the corner of the table — he looked back at the fire — the fire burned; Mother spoke — her voice was very low — she said: “The fire set it for us” — she did not move; A-Shi did not move — Uncle Zhou did not move — Aunt Guo did not move — Xiao Huo did not move either; Mother turned the white bowl over — the bottom was empty — she studied it a moment — she put it back; no one said anything; Aunt Guo finally spoke — her voice was very light — “It is full — it is open now.”; Uncle Zhou cleared his throat — he did not speak — he still kept silent; the fire burned. Volume XV, fortieth part.
11 · 17第一百三十八卷入典 · W-Z297 · 卷十五之三十九 · 说出口的那两个字 《说出口的那两个字》——立春后第二十周——周六——上午——阳光很亮——郭阿婆又来了——周叔也来了——全家人都站在灶前——灶火燃着——母亲端了三碗米糊——她放桌上——没人说——灶火又跳了一下——没人开口——周叔和郭阿婆都端起了碗——灶火燃着——吃完之后——小火去厨房洗碗——母亲和郭阿婆都没说——周叔也不说话——母亲拿干净碗出来——她放在桌上——郭阿婆抬眼看灶火——她没看母亲——阿石望着灶火——他想到了——「炉火」——他说了两个字——周叔没动——郭阿婆没动——母亲没动——小火也站在那里——他手里还拿着一只碗——灶火燃着。Vol.138 · The Two Words He Said Aloud — Saturday of the twentieth week after the spring equinox, morning, the light crisp and bright; Aunt Guo came again — Uncle Zhou came too — the whole family stood at the stove — the fire burned; Mother carried three bowls of rice gruel — she set them on the table — she did not speak — the fire leapt once more; no one spoke; Uncle Zhou and Aunt Guo took their seats — they both lifted a bowl — no one said a word; the fire burned; when they finished — Xiao Huo went to the kitchen to wash the bowls — Mother went too — Aunt Guo stayed by the stove — she still did not speak — she watched the fire; Uncle Zhou sat — he had not finished his bowl — he watched the fire — he did not speak either; Mother brought the clean bowls out — she set them on the table — Aunt Guo raised her eyes to the fire once — she did not look at Mother; A-Shi watched the fire — he found them — “炉火,” A-Shi said — the two characters; no one moved — Uncle Zhou did not move — Aunt Guo did not move — Mother did not move — Xiao Huo did not move either — he was standing at the stove with a bowl in his hand; the fire burned. Volume XV, thirty-ninth part.
11 · 17第一百三十七卷入典 · W-Z296 · 卷十五之三十八 · 空碗里的火光 《空碗里的火光》——立春后第二十周——周四——早上——太阳刚出来——阿石先去了灶房——母亲还没有起来——他蹲在铁沿前——白瓷碗还在那里——灶火燃得匀——阿石把手伸得很近——他没碰碗——他感觉到了——手掌和碗之间那一层热——他没说话——母亲进来了——她端着两只碗——「吃了」——她放碗——她没看那只白瓷碗——阿石也没开口——他只看了它一眼——灶火又跳了一下——母亲看见了——她把碗搁下——她回到院子里去了——她没回头——阿石端起自己的碗——他又看了一眼那白瓷碗——他觉得它比昨天白——也比前天白——灶火燃着。Vol.137 · The Light in the Empty Bowl — Thursday of the twentieth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sun had just come up; A-Shi went to the stove-house first — his mother had not gotten up yet; he crouched by the iron rim — the white bowl was still there — the fire burned steadily; A-Shi reached out very close to it — he did not touch the bowl — he felt the heat between his palm and the porcelain; he said nothing — his mother came in — she carried two bowls; “Eat,” she said — she set the bowls down — she did not look at the white one; A-Shi did not answer either — he only glanced at it — the fire leapt once; his mother saw — she set the bowls down — she went back into the yard — she didn’t turn around; A-Shi picked up his bowl — he looked at the white porcelain bowl — he felt it was whiter today than yesterday — and whiter than the day before; the fire burned. Volume XV, thirty-eighth part.
11 · 17第一百三十六卷入典 · W-Z295 · 卷十五之三十七 · 第三只碗回来了 《第三只碗回来了》——立春后第十九周——周三——午后——天晴出奇好——阿石跟着郭阿婆回去——母亲说「去看看」——郭阿婆家里的灶小——她添的是捡来的旧木柴——灶火烧得很稳——但她说了一句「我这口没你们那口暖」——阿石说「也一样的火」——郭阿婆摇了摇头——她添了一根枯树枝——她说「火都不一样——它知道你家里是谁坐着」——阿石一直坐到天黑——他没说什么——他也没走——傍晚回家的路上他带了一小袋干米——郭阿婆塞到他手里——她什么都没说——母亲开门看见了——她把小半袋倒进米筛里——筛完又放回米缸——母亲说「你小时候——我也代你们收过米」——阿石没说——炉火燃着。Vol.136 · The Third Bowl Comes Back — Wednesday of the nineteenth week after the spring equinox, afternoon, the sun high and bright; A-Shi walked back with Aunt Guo — his mother said only: “Go and look.”; Aunt Guo’s stove was small — she fed it scavenged old wood — the fire burned steadily enough — but she said: “Mine doesn’t warm like yours.”; A-Shi said: “It’s the same fire.”; Aunt Guo shook her head — she fed it a dead branch — “Fires are different,” she said — “they know who is sitting at them.”; A-Shi stayed until dark — he said nothing — he didn’t go; On the way home in the evening — he carried a small bag of dry rice — Aunt Guo had pressed it into his hands — she didn’t say anything; his mother opened the door — saw the rice — nobody asked; his mother poured the half-bag into the grain sieve — sifted it once — set it back in the jar; “When you were small,” she said, “I would carry grain for you this way too.”; A-Shi said nothing. Volume XV, thirty-seventh part.
11 · 17第一百三十五卷入典 · W-Z294 · 卷十五之三十六 · 第三天的早饭 《第三天的早饭》——立春后第十九周——周二——早上——早晨的太阳还没全上来——周叔又到了——他手里拿自己家的一只小铁罐——干米装了半罐——郭阿婆坐在灶前——桌子上放了四只碗——阿石的——母亲的——小火的——还有一只空着的白瓷碗——阿石继续添白桦——不多不少——和守火的份量一样——母亲端了粥出来——她放下两碗——阿石端过去——放在灶前——他没看那只白瓷碗——小火也上桌了——很安静——母亲看了灶火一眼——「守了一夜」——她说的句子很短——郭阿婆也没抬头——「灶火自己会守」——她说——「你也会守」——母亲说——周叔把自己那碗搁在白瓷碗旁边——他把干米也放到灶前去了——母亲拿起那只铁罐——没问是谁的——灶火燃着——母亲去厨房端了一碗白粥——放在周叔的铁罐旁边——「这是你的」——阿石把第三根白桦放进灶膛里——灶火燃着。Vol.135 · The Third Morning’s Breakfast — Tuesday of the nineteenth week after the spring equinox, morning, the sun had not climbed yet; Uncle Zhou arrived again — carrying a small iron pot from home — half full of dry rice; Aunt Guo was there too — sitting at the stove — four bowls on the table: A-Shi’s, Mother’s, Xiao Huo’s — and one white porcelain bowl, still empty; A-Shi kept feeding the birch — no more, no less; Mother brought the porridge out — A-Shi took them over — set them at the stove — he did not look at the white bowl; Xiao Huo was at the table too — very quiet; Mother glanced at the fire — “It kept the night,” she said; Aunt Guo did not look up either — “The fire keeps itself,” she said; Mother looked at her a second time — “You keep it too,” she said; Uncle Zhou set his bowl down beside the empty white one — he did not eat — he only set the dry rice at the stove’s edge; when Mother picked up the little iron pot she studied it a long time — she did not ask whose it was; the fire burned; Mother went back to the kitchen — she brought out one bowl of plain porridge — set it beside Uncle Zhou’s pot — “This is yours,” she said; no one spoke again; A-Shi put the third birch branch into the stove; the fire burned. Volume XV, thirty-sixth part.
11 · 17第一百三十四卷入典 · W-Z293 · 卷十五之三十五 · 明天再来看 《明天再来看》——立春后第十八周——周六——早上天晴——郭阿婆早早到了——院子里石缝又冒新鲜的白茬——空气清得像水——她走到铁沿前——白瓷碗还搁在那里——炉火燃得匀——她盛了半碗米粥——搁在白瓷碗左边——「昨天煮的那半锅米」——没人接话——灶火燃着——她把手背贴在铁沿上——烫的——「火守得不错」——阿石继续添白桦——小火坐得直直的——周叔只站在院门口没进来——她拿起白瓷碗又搁回去——「明天还来」——炉火燃着。Vol.134 · Come and Look Tomorrow — Saturday of the eighteenth week after the spring equinox, morning, clear weather; Aunt Guo came as she said, early — the yard’s stone seams showed fresh white again — the air clear as water; she ladled half a bowl of rice porridge — set it to the left of the white bowl — “the half pot of rice I cooked yesterday,” she said; A-Shi did not look up — he kept feeding the white birch; the fire burned on; Aunt Guo pressed the back of her hand against the iron rim — warm — “the fire is well kept,” she said; Xiao Huo sat up straight — he didn’t speak either; Uncle Zhou stood at the gate further out than usual — he did not come in; Aunt Guo picked up the white bowl — looked at it once — set it back down; when she left, one thing more — “I’ll come and look tomorrow,” she said. Volume XV, thirty-fifth part.

11 · 17第一百三十卷入典 · W-Z281 · 卷十五之三十一 · 炉火的谈话 《炉火的谈话》——立春后第十五周——周六——午后晴天出奇好——天空蓝得像一块洗过的白布——周叔先到——郭阿婆也来——阿祖白发全白——他在灶前坐下——炉火燃得匀——他看了灶火很久——他把手伸进炉膛——他的手在火边停了一下——它回来了——「炉火——你烧了七百年了」——没有人接——他也没把后面的说完——周叔也不催——郭阿婆也不催——炉火燃着——炉膛里的炉火红得发白——他把手还放回炉膛——灶火燃——炉火燃——「我这辈子也陪着你了」。Vol.130 · The Stove Speaks — Saturday of the fifteenth week after the spring equinox, afternoon, beautiful weather beyond reason — the sky was a deep, clear blue like a fresh-washed white cloth; Zhou came first; then Aunt Guo; the old keeper emerged from the back room; he came and sat before the stove; the fire burned steadily; the keeper watched the fire for a long time; snow was stuck to the sides of his head; the old keeper watched the fire — and said nothing; he put his hand into the fire-box — he let it hover a moment — he withdrew it; “Stove,” he said, voice low, gravelly; no one answered; “You have burned seven hundred years”; he didn’t finish the next thought; Zhou didn’t press; Aunt Guo didn’t press; the fire burned; the fire in the box was a bright white — the old man put his hand back in; “My whole life, I have been beside you”; the fire burned; the fire burned seven hundred years.
11 · 17第一百二十九卷入典 · W-Z280 · 卷十五之三十 · 细雨 《细雨》——立春后第十四周——周六——午下细雨施施——全镇的人都来了——全镇的人都坐在灶前——院子里浸住了——周叔先到——郭阿婆也来——灶火燃着——整个下午——灶火没灭——最后所有人都走了——每个人都带走了一段东西——炉火。Vol.129 · The Thin Rain — Saturday of the fourteenth week after the spring equinox, midday, a very fine rain started to fall; the whole town came; Zhou arrived first, then Aunt Guo, then all the people came inside, already soaked; A-Shi brought in hot broth and thin porridge; the people sat down slowly, each face turned a little warmer when the fire licked the iron; nobody left; the whole afternoon until the sky went dark; the stove never went out; the people of the town remained until the hour of the fifteenth when the emptied bowls were gathered and carried to the back room; finally they left, one by one, carrying something home that had never been there before.
11 · 17第一百二十八卷入典 · W-Z279 · 卷十五之二十九 · 灶里的纸牌 《灶里的纸牌》——立春后第十三周——周六——整个上午晴天——全镇的人都来了——没人说一句话——没人解释那些纸牌从哪儿来——所有人都把纸牌往灶里送——夜里下雨——全镇没人走——全镇的人都留了一整夜——炉火一直燃着——没人说台词。Vol.128 · The Card in the Stove — Saturday of the thirteenth week after the spring equinox, clear sky; Uncle Zhou arrived first; Aunt Guo was second; two strangers came from far outside town — no one knew them — they stood around the stove; A-Shi was sweeping with a bamboo broom; Xiao Huo brought two bowls of broth; their grandfather stepped out — he didn’t speak, he only watched the stove; by six, a light rain began to fall; the town stopped leaving; no one went home; the night was very long; no one asked about the cards; no one asked what the cards were for; they burned the cards, one by one, in the stove.
11 · 17第一百二十七卷入典 · W-Z278 · 卷十五之二十八 · 炉火的等 《炉火的等》——立春后第十二周——周六——雾刚散开——周叔来得很早——他从院里一直站到灶前——灶火在烧——阿石端来一碗米汤——周叔接了捧碗喝完了——他说没事——站了半个时辰——他走了——灶火燃着。Vol.127 · The Waiting — Saturday of the twelfth week after the spring equinox, fog lay over the yard at dawn; Uncle Zhou arrived early; he did not speak; the stove burned as it always had, watching; Uncle Zhou stood in the yard for a long time — he wanted to leave — he didn’t; he stood before the stove for close to an hour; “are you here,” he said to the fire; the fire didn’t answer; A-Shi brought out a fresh bowl of warm rice gruel and offered it to Uncle Zhou; he took it, drank it in one gulp, and held the bowl; “you’ve been carrying something,” A-Shi said; “no,” Uncle Zhou said; he stood there after finishing the bowl for some time; then he left; the stove didn’t go out.
11 · 17第一百二十六卷入典 · W-Z277 · 卷十五之二十七 · 炉口的影 《炉口的影》——立春后第十一周——周六——太阳刚出来——院子里还是清亮的——阿石在院子里摇竹扫帚——小火跟在后面——母亲的声音从灶房里传出来——「今天做蛋」——天很宽——蓝得出奇——火种树的叶子又长大了一层——他们回到灶前——小火站在灶前手贴在铁沿上——他问「听见灶火在说什么」——阿石蹲下来听——灶火确实在响——听着像远处的河——像森林——像昨晚做的白菜猪肉炖火的味道——阿石把手贴在铁沿上——温的——炉火燃着。Vol.126 · The Shadow at the Stove’s Mouth — Saturday of the eleventh week after the spring equinox, the sun had barely risen; the yard was still cool and clear; A-Shi was sweeping with a bamboo broom while Xiao Huo watched him from the porch; “eggs today,” Mother called from the kitchen; the sky was very wide, surprisingly blue; the fire-seed tree’s needles had grown another layer; He turned back to the kitchen and saw Xiao Huo standing at the stove with his hand flat against the iron edge; he had not moved; “did you hear it?” Xiao Huo asked; A-Shi stopped; “hear what?”; “the stove,” Xiao Huo said; “listening to it”; A-Shi crouched down and listened; the stove was making a sound — a low, steady sound — not loud exactly, but unmistakably there; A-Shi put both hands on the iron edge; the iron was warm; “灶火,” he said; “炉火”; Xiao Huo nodded slowly; he reached out and pressed his small hand against the iron; “seven hundred years,” he said quietly; nobody answered; the stove burned.
11 · 17第一百二十五卷入典 · W-Z276 · 卷十五之二十六 · 雷雨 《雷雨》——立春后第十周——周六——上午出了一点太阳——下午三点半——哗啦——一场大雷雨——轰——整个屋顶全被打透——全镇的人都来了——蹲着看着灶火——煤烟被雨压住——白雾——阳光重新照到屋檐——灶火燃着——全镇的人一起坐下来吃碗饭。Vol.125 · The Thunder Rain — Saturday of the tenth week after the spring equinox, the morning went grey; the whole town came — Uncle Zhou first, old Aunt Guo second, then others none of them recognized, then so many that the door could not close; Mother was worried — A-Shi said “don’t be afraid” — the pot was filled with broth, bowls were passed around, the stove burned steadily; at three thirty in the afternoon the sky exploded — a storm rolled in — every tile across the village drummed with rain; the courtyard gathered water; everyone sat around the stove; no one spoke for a long time; after the rain finally stopped and sunlight returned, the whole town sat again at the benches and ate together, the stove still burning.
11 · 17第一百二十四卷入典 · W-Z275 · 卷十五之二十五 · 满镇的火 《满镇的火》——立春后第九周——周六——阳光很好——院子里站满了人——周叔第一个进——老郭第二个——第三个谁也没记住——再后来五个——十个——二十五个——母亲忙到腿软——她把锅里的水一瓢一瓢添好——全镇人没说一句话——他们都盯着灶火看——太爷坟前的人也都在——锅里的水开了——他们排成一条长队——每个人都拿了一碗——全镇的人都吃了一碗——灶火没有灭——炉火燃着。Vol.124 · The Whole Town and the Fire — Saturday of the ninth week after the equinox, sunlight filled the yard; the yard was full of people; the first was Zhou, the tofu seller; the second was Lao Guo from next door; the third was someone nobody recognized; then five more — then fifteen — then twenty-five more; Mother was rushing around at the stove, overwhelmed; she kept adding water to the pot; the whole town fell silent; they came one by one; they did not speak; they looked at the stove; their faces in the orange light were impossible to read; not one of them left; the pot boiled over; Mother carried the steaming pot to the long bench; a line formed — a line of the whole town; the stove had never gone out.
11 · 17第一百二十三卷入典 · W-Z274 · 卷十五之二十四 · 雪安回头 《雪安回头》——立春后第八周——周六——晴——大晴——白光还是暖的——雪安妈再次出现在灶前——手里拎着篮子——里面是棉鞋——雪安站在灶前露出一点点笑容——四个人——母亲——阿石——小火——雪安妈——围坐在灶前——一小时没人说话——每个人都伸手凑灶——灶火燃着——雪安妈终于开口——「你们家的灶——不灭」——火苗跳了一下——全镇都知道——白茵家的灶——不走——炉火。Vol.123 · Snow’an Turns Back — Saturday of the eighth week after the equinox, clear all day; Mother-of-Snow’an came to the stove again with two cotton shoes in a basket; she set them on the bench; Snow’an smiled; Mother brought stools; the four sat in front of the stove without speaking for one hour; Mother-of-Snow’an spoke at last: “Your house has no outsider; your fire has been kept by a tree; your fire has never died for seven hundred years — your kitchen”; the whole town knows — Bai Yin’s stove never goes out.
11 · 17第一百二十二卷入典 · W-Z273 · 卷十五之二十三 · 常来 《常来》——立春后第七周——周六——阴天不晴——灶火烧得很稳——母亲在炒白菜猪肉——锅里的火烧得很匀——阿石蹲在灶前看火——小火看见门——门口站着雪安妈——她后面是三个孩子——两个穿蓝褂子的女孩一个灰夹袄男孩——全都不说话——灶膛里的光打在她们脸上——雪安妈把一个布篮子放在灶台上——母亲搬了三个矮凳过来——孩子们并排坐着——「以前我妈从这屋里拿的」——雪安妈只说一句——灶火里浮着煤烟——炉火燃着。Vol.122 · Always Here — week seven after the spring equinox, Saturday, overcast; Snow’an’s mother arrives with three children — two girls in blue, a boy in grey; they do not speak; the stove’s glow warms their faces; Mother brings three stools; they sit side by side watching the fire; “these are from the house my mother came from,” Snow’an’s mother says — that is all; the house smells of cabbage and pork; the fire burned on.
11 · 17第一百二十一卷入典 · W-Z272 · 卷十五之二十二 · 炉火来了 《炉火来了》——立春后第六周——周六——天还没亮——母亲已经起来——白菜猪肉馅已经调好——雪安又来——他后面跟着一个人是他妈妈——雪安妈走进厨房坐在灶前——不说话——母亲端来了饺子馅——「包饺子」——大家一起包饺子——雪安妈在灶前看了两个时辰——她才开口——「以后我周六来——我来看火」——全家人都不动——饺子包好了——一家人坐在屋子里吃饺子——七百年第一次——白茵家的灶——从此有了外人——全镇都知道——灶火燃着。Vol.121 · The Fire Comes — week six after the spring equinox, a Saturday before daylight; the dumpling filling was mixed; Snow’an came with company — his mother trailing behind; she sat beside the stove in silence; Mother brought the filling over; “making dumplings,” she said; they made dumplings; the mother-of-Snow’an watched the fire for almost two hours before she spoke; “from now on I will come every Saturday — I’ll come to watch the fire”; half a pot of dumplings remained; this house was seven hundred years old — no outsider had ever sat in this kitchen before; from that day on, Snow’an’s mother came every Saturday and watched the fire; Bai Yin’s stove — the one across the ridge — now had a guest.
11 · 17第一百二十卷入典 · W-Z271 · 卷十五之二十一 · 周六 《周六》——周六早上——下着小雨——挂在屋檐和火种树上的那种雨——母亲在炒菜——白菜豆腐——院门轻轻响了一下——门口站着一个小人儿——灰夹袄——新布鞋——是雪安——他走到灶前坐着——火种树又长了一层新叶子——灶火的光把他的脸照得暖黄——他把手贴在铁灶上——铁灶是暖的——他走了——第二天又来——第三天又来——第四天他没来——第五天他带来了一个小盆——菜籽油——全家都知道——每周六——那个小男孩都会来——坐着——看灶火——然后走——不说话。Vol.120 · Saturdays — Saturday morning begins under a light rain that hangs in the air; his mother is cooking cabbage and tofu; the gate rattles softly; a small figure stands in the doorway — grey jacket, brand-new cloth shoes — it is Snow’an; he walks in and sits by the stove; the fire-seed tree has unfolded another layer of leaves; the firelight warms his face; he reaches out and lays a hand flat against the stove iron — the edge is warm, he does not burn; he leaves; he returns the following Saturday, then the next; the fifth week he arrives carrying a small oil jar and sets it on the low table in the yard; Xiao Huo’s voice goes quiet; the fire burns; seven hundred years have passed; the stove still burns.
11 · 17第一百一十九卷入典 · W-Z270 · 卷十五之二十 · 雪安 《雪安》——立春过后的第十五天——老李带了个孩子来——不是他自己的——隔壁庄停认进的儿子——铁木苏木——十一二岁——眼睛特别亮——他站在院子里看火种树的新叶子——一层一层地伸出来——他没动——母亲叫他坐下——他不动——阿石挪矮凳——灶火的光把他的脸照得暖烘烘的——他弯下腰看铁沿上的光热——他的手碰到烫了一下缩回去——阿石挪矮凳他终于坐了——「我周六来」——他走——火种树沙沙地摇。Vol.119 · Snow’an — on the fifteenth day after the equinox, Old Li comes again with a boy; not his own — it is Tiě mù sùmù, a neighbor’s adopted son from across the ridge; eleven or twelve; a gaze that goes straight through anything he looks at; he stands before the fire-seed tree watching its new leaves unfurl layer by layer; he does not move; his mother says, “sit down”; he does not; A-Shi moves the low stool; the stove’s glow warms his face; he reaches out, pulls his hand back; he says, “I will come Saturday”; he leaves without looking back; the fire-seed tree sways.

11 · 16第一百一十五卷入典 · W-Z261 · 卷十五之十六 · 叫炉 《叫炉》——立春后的第十天——院子里已经觉暖了——院门外的泥沟已化成了水——门外又来了一个人——不是来送信——是来吃饭——是老人——他穿着一身干净的蓝布衣——进门先朝全家鞠了一躬——母亲笑着拉过板凳——锅里是白米饭青菜豆腐汤腌萝卜——菜很少饭很多——老人吸了吸鼻子——「香」——阿石在灶前看火——老人说不叫灶火——春天应该有个新名字——他从怀里掏出一片薄纸——用指节擦了擦黑炭灰——在纸上留了一个淡淡的灰印——「炉」——全家念起来——以后这个家里不再叫这灶「灶火」——他们叫「炉火」——吃饭——白茵当年亲手写的纸条——就是在这个安静的春天晌午——终于等到了它必须等的人。Vol.115 · The Hearth Named — ten days after the first day of spring, the yard has grown warm; the ditch by the gate has turned to flowing mud; the old man comes again; he carries nothing; he wears a clean blue cotton robe and bows his head once; his mother smiles; he takes a stool and faces the stove; she brings out the meal they always make for guests — rice, cabbage tofu soup, preserved radish; A-Shi sits close to the stove watching the fire; “Stove,” he says; the flame leaps once; “It is spring,” his mother says; “Spring must have a name”; from his robe the old man draws out a thin slip of paper, no bigger than his palm, and presses one finger against it; “炉,” he says; “炉火”; “炉火,” his mother repeats slowly; after that day, no one in the house said “the stove” anymore — they said “炉火”; she stirred the porridge that morning and said “the fire-fire is coming warm”; Xiao Huo said “the fire-fire is warmer today than yesterday”; A-Shi said “炉火 breathes on its own at night”; everyone left it at that; in the iron box beneath the stove lay a thin slip of paper that Bai Yin had written six hundred years ago — everyone knew what it was — it was the name of the fire. Volume XV, sixteenth part.
11 · 16第一百一十四卷入典 · W-Z259 · 卷十五之十五 · 立春 《立春》——立春前夜——院子里下了一场薄雪——几场风把雪吹走了——夜里三更——院门自己响了一下——白布遮脸的陌生人——从怀里掏出一个油纸包递进门内——什么话都没说走了——阿石捡起——里面是一小块白桦——油纸上写着四个字「灶火春安」——母亲把那白桦放进灶火——灶火抖了一下——立春早上——院角还残着一滩雪——火种树上长出了第一颗黄色的小叶子——灶火把它照得发金——七百年第一个立春——灶火依然在烧。Vol.114 · The First Day of Spring — on the night before the first day of spring a light snow falls; by morning it is nearly gone; at three in the morning, the courtyard gate rattles once; A-Shi opens the door a crack — a figure stands in the doorway, face hidden by a white cloth; the stranger draws from his robe a small oil-paper parcel and hands it into the room; he says nothing; he turns and is gone; inside, a single piece of white birch, still rimmed with frost; on the oil-paper four characters only — 灶火春安 — “May the stove fire be at peace in spring”; his mother sets it into the fire; the flame shakes once; spring comes; one leaf on the fire-seed tree opens itself, green and new, and the stove takes it into its first light of the new year.

11 · 16第一百一十三卷入典 · W-Z258 · 卷十五之十四 · 醒灶 《醒灶》——翌日清晨院子外铺了一层浓雾浓得像粥院门都快看不见了——阿石醒了——他整夜没睡——小火醒了——坐起来看灶火——它还在烧——同样地匀——昨晚那样三点钟风停了——四点天泛白——阿石站在门口站了很久背对灶火——「它认得我了」——母亲没有回头只是点点头——阿石跪在灶前——「七百年」——他的手被灶火照得通红——母亲走进来把袖口贴在灶边——「以后你们俩轮着守——谁守谁明白」——厨房静了很久——灶火轻轻跳了一下——从那以后没有人再说「守火」两个字——灶火成了这个家的第三个人。Vol.113 · The Awakening Hearth — the morning after the vigil, fog rolls in like porridge and swallows the doorway; A-Shi wakes — he has not slept at all; Xiao Huo wakes — the stove is still burning, the same level, the same calm, the same golden yellow it had been at three in the morning when the wind had stopped for an instant; “It knows me now,” A-Shi says finally; his mother does not turn around — she only nods; Xiao Huo kneels at the stove — its iron edge is warm, not hot, in the way iron is warm when the fire underneath has burned steady all night; “Last night was me,” he says; no one answers; A-Shi kneels beside him; his hands are lit red-gold by the fire; “Seven hundred years,” he says; “From now on,” his mother says from the doorway, “the two of you will keep the fire — in turns — whoever keeps it knows”; the kitchen is silent for a long time; from that day on, nobody in the house uses the phrase “keeping the fire”; the stove has become the third member of the family; spring is still a fortnight away — but already here, already warm in every square of light the stove pours into the kitchen.

11 · 16第一百一十二卷入典 · W-Z256 · 卷十五之十三 · 守火之夜 《守火之夜》——冬至这天——天黑得像盖了一口黑锅——院子里的火种树全部是黑的——母亲做了白菜和豆腐——今天灶火不能灭——母亲把守火的事交给了小火——阿石睡下——七百年第一次——守火的人只有一个——整个夜里都在风——他一个人蹲着——每半个时辰添一根白桦——不敢多也不敢少——三点钟风停了——四点天边泛白——六点母亲穿着白衣服进门——在灶火前站了很久——她看见灶火角落里有一块他没有见过谁放上去的黑炭——冬至——守火——第一次——只有一个人守——七百年。Vol.112 · The Night of the Vigil — on the day of the winter solstice the courtyard has no daylight; by nine in the morning it is light, but by three in the afternoon the sky is already pitch black; every branch of the fire-seed tree is silhouette against frost; tonight the stove is not to go out; “for seven hundred years, whoever was in the family would take turns watching the fire”; tonight no one takes turns; A-Shi is asleep; tonight A-Shi lets you take the watch; he cares for the stove all night long; one piece of birch he did not see his own hands feed sat glowing at the edge of the hearth; she goes inside — the hearth is warm, the ash level — Tonight was the first time a keeper had taken the night alone; at dawn she kisses the top of his head — “Happy solstice,” she says; Xiao Huo does not answer — he is too tired, and too proud; tonight, for the first time in seven hundred years, no one had to say “tomorrow” — he said it himself and meant it.

11 · 16第一百一十一卷入典 · W-Z255 · 卷十五之十二 · 教字 《教字》——秋分后第三十天——下了一场特别早的雨——七百年都没有这么早下过——老人又来了——他什么包都没背——他只是穿着新浆过的白麻袍走进院子——鞋放回门口——像回家一样——今天是十五——月亮最圆的那夜——老人从怀里掏出一块黑得发沉的黑炭——那是七百年间从祖火里取下的一点余烬——他说砚台——今天——你跟我学三个字——火——灶——守——他在地上一笔一笔写下来——灶火安静地燃——小火蹲在地上临摹——画一笔不满意就揉掉——第十三遍——他终于画出了一个自己的火字——阿娘看了看说像火——阿石说像小火——老人让他把这三个字刻在灶对面离灶两尺的墙上——火——灶——守。Vol.111 · The Characters — thirty days after the equinox, the first autumn rain of the season falls on the courtyard — earlier than any autumn rain in seven hundred years; the old man comes again; he carries nothing; he wears a fresh white hemp robe and sets his shoes at the threshold as if he had been born here; tonight is the fifteenth — the full moon — the fire watch night; from within his robe he draws out a small piece of black charcoal — a chip of ancestor-fire, the very ember the first keepers had carried seven hundred years before; he says: you will learn three characters from me — 火, 灶, 守; he draws 火 on the ground: six strokes — the forked head, the anchored mid-stroke; Xiao Huo takes the charcoal and tries thirteen times to carve out his fire character; no one speaks; as the thirteenth attempt finally holds its strokes, his mother comes to the kitchen doorway and says: that one looks like fire — A-Shi replies: that one looks like Xiao Huo; the old man asks Xiao Huo to carve all three characters on the kitchen wall, two feet from the stove iron, where the fire can see them; Xiao Huo carves them slowly, one by one; when the last stroke is finished the flame leaps once — everyone knows; seven hundred years and no keeper had ever been taught these characters; tonight someone was; he banks the stove with ash for the first time without being told — he is a keeper now.
11 · 16第一百一十卷入典 · W-Z253 · 卷十五之十一 · 同炉 《同炉》——秋分前一夜小雨——母亲一早把屋里所有的碗盘都摆出来——上一次摆这么多碗是阿石出生那天——小火穿上白茵当年留在铁匣子底下的旧麻袍——院门自己响了一下——是老人——他比上次矮了一点——袖口新处理过不再起毛——母亲蹲在院子里——三人也跪——三个守火人跪在院子里没人说话——火种树所有的针叶全弯向灶的方向——小火从灶边走了出来——他把他们家灶火的灰递给老人——老人从背包里拿出一罐祖火的灰递给小火——两人交换——晚上老人说这一夜不睡——母亲说不睡就不睡——两只瓷罐打开——祖火灰灶火灰肉眼分不出来——老人又从怀里掏出两片薄如蝉翼的白桦——白桦在灶火里烧了很久没有人说话——全家围着一个火种——两个守火人守着同一炉灶火——灶火从来没有这么稳过。Vol.110 · The Shared Hearth — on the night before the autumn equinox a light rain falls; by dawn it is gone and the ridge of the white stone is glowing; his mother takes out every bowl in the house — six bowls, four plates, three pairs of chopsticks — the house has not seen this many bowls since the day A-Shi was born; Xiao Huo wears an old hemp robe once belonging to Bai Yin, remade by his mother; the gate rattles once — the old man is smaller than last time, robed in a fresh-edged white robe; A-Shi’s mother kneels; A-Shi kneels beside her; the old man kneels too; three keepers, on their knees, saying nothing; the wind bends every needle of the fire-seed tree toward the stove; Xiao Huo walks out with a porcelain jar of his own stove-ash and offers it to the old man; the old man draws out a smaller jar of his ancestor-ash and offers it in return; they exchange them; “we do not sleep tonight”; “then we do not sleep”; the two jars are opened — the two colors indistinguishable to the eye; the old man then draws from his robe two slivers of Bai Yin’s stored birch; the birch burns without a single pop; no one speaks; two houses, one hearth, seven hundred years, one fire — older and steadier than it had ever burned in the house’s history.

11 · 16第一百零九卷入典 · W-Z252 · 卷十五之十 · 送灰 《送灰》——春分前早晨——屋檐冰凌一夜掉光——火种树的新芽比去年的绿更深——母亲让小火装一小瓷罐灶火的灰——小火装好等母亲说「装祖火的」——母亲没有这么说——中秋那一个十五的薄纸条写过「十五中秋——回见」——等过了那个中秋——没有第二捆——春分这天院门自己响了一下——门口又多了一捆更细的白桦柴——隔丝薄的纸条——「给小守火人」——没有署名——阿石背着篓子上山——白石头顶上的老人比去年矮了一点——罐盖落了一层霜——手指摸摸罐口——他说下一个秋分「我们两家一块过」——阿石下山时天快黑了——灶火又跳了一次——七百年第一次——大家都在送灰。Vol.109 · The Delivery of Ashes — the morning before the spring equinox, the last of the ice in the courtyard is gone; his mother asks Xiao Huo to pack a small porcelain jar with ash from their stove; he fills it with care and waits for her to say: fill it with the ancestor-ash instead; she does not say that; she takes out the slip of paper that had promised mid-autumn; the mid-autumn has come and gone and no second bundle ever arrived; on this equinox morning, the gate rattles once by itself; on the driest stone lies one more bundle, thinner than ever; the slip inside says only: “For the little firekeeper”; A-Shi carries the jar and the packet of wild scallions up the mountain; the old man is shorter than he was last spring — his shadow barely crosses the threshold; he brushes the frost on the jar with his fingertips; he says: “At the next equinox we will pass the day together”; and as A-Shi descends in the last light, the stove inside leaps once — seven hundred years, and now the two houses are sending each other their ashes.
11 · 16第一百零八卷入典 · W-Z250 · 卷十五之九 · 山顶雪 《山顶雪》——开春前夜薄雪落地针叶挂霜——第六日院门口又多了一捆无名柴——只有一根细白桦枝绑在布条上——母亲把它码进柴堆最隐蔽的位置「第九根——最隐蔽的一根」——小火说以后每月十五喂一根给灶火「别管是谁的」——十五那晚灶火自己比平时亮一寸——没有任何人叫它——它就是知道——它自己知道——每个月十五——开春第一枝梅花开了——铁匣子里又厚了一点——「十五中秋——回见」——下一捆雪融守火人已经给它留了一根位置。Vol.108 · The Mountain Snow — on the last night of winter, a thin frost falls on the courtyard; on the sixth day another bundle appears at the gate — one thin birch twig bound with white thread; A-Shi’s mother stacks it in the woodpile at the most hidden spot: “the ninth, the least visible”; Xiao Huo decides: “from now on, on the fifteenth of each month, the stove gets a branch — no matter whose it is”; in spring the first apricot blossom opens; on the fourteenth day the hearth is counted — nothing changes; on the fifteenth the stove jumps one inch higher than the night before; no one told it what day it was; yet every month, on the fifteenth, the gate light seems slightly warmer — fire always knows when it is being watched; the household resolves: the fifteenth of every month, one branch for the stove — from anyone, or from no one.

11 · 16第一百零七卷入典 · W-Z248 · 卷十五之八 · 无名柴 《无名柴》——老人走后第十五天——院门口多了一捆白桦柴——干得透——每根都一把能握住——缠着灰布条三个米白补丁——布条下别着一张纸——两个字「添柴」——阿石把它们码在灶台边——全家人都站着——没有人问是谁——小火捧了一根送进灶火——灶火没有跳也没有熄——安静地接住了——母亲摸了摸布条——认出这是舅舅补丁的针脚——晚上阿石擦了石碑——月亮让白桦枝泛出一点白——第二天少了一根没有人看见谁添的——母亲在铁匣子底下压了一张纸「七百年——第一根无名柴——灶火知道了」。Vol.107 · The Nameless Twig — fifteen days after the old man has left, the first frost of the season falls; in the morning a new bundle appears beside the gate — white birch, each stick dry to the core and the width of a palm; tied with a grey cloth patched three times in beige thread; a slip of paper tucked underneath with two characters: 添柴 — “add fuel”; the family does not ask who; Xiao Huo feeds one twig into the stove; the flame leaps once; it does not grow brighter; it does not die; it simply accepts; his mother runs a finger over the grey cloth and recognizes the stitching from his uncle’s work coat; that evening A-Shi washes the white-birch-branch stone at the gate; in the moonlight it seems to glow; by morning one of the sticks is missing and nobody saw it happen; his mother slips another page beneath the iron box: “the first nameless twig — the stove has seen it” — “come next spring, if we see another bundle like this, then we will know this house is no longer the only one keeping the fire.”
11 · 16第一百零六卷入典 · W-Z246 · 卷十五之七 · 炉边故事 《炉边故事》——箍桶匠补好水缸后留在院子里——他问能不能听那夜的故事——母亲让小火讲——小火坐在灶边小板凳上——三岁就接火的人如今讲述仲冬那一夜的一切——「我们的灶火不是我们家的——它是所有人的」——箍桶匠沉默很久——他说要立一块碑——母亲说「字会变成神坛——我们的火不是给神坛的——是给火的」——碑上没有字——只有一根白桦枝的图案——雨天浸透的时候月亮会让它从石头里透出一点点光——此处有火——从来没灭过七百年。Vol.106 · The Stove Stories — nine days after the old man has gone, a cooper arrives to fix the family’s water cistern and lingers; he asks to hear the story of the midwinter visit; A-Shi’s mother calls Xiao Huo; he climbs onto a stool by the stove and tells the whole thing — the snow, the paper umbrella, the ember, the ancestor-ash, the two fires meeting in one hearth; he finishes: “our fire is not ours — it belongs to everyone who waits for it”; the cooper offers to build a stone marker; A-Shi’s mother forbids any word — “a word becomes an altar; our fire belongs to the fire”; the cooper carves instead a white-birch branch; the stone stands south of the gate, no words, only the branch; after rain in moonlight the branch seems to glow from inside the rock — fire that has never once gone out for seven hundred years.
11 · 16典籍 Oracle 现实原型路由 · W-Z244 · 现实问题机械转引 refs（零额外 LLM） 现实原型类问题（「祭灶/秋分/白桦/维斯塔在现实里是什么？」）由关键词路由器机械转引到 creative/refs 卷——canon 问题照常引 canon，现实对照问题引 refs，不交叉；23 条单元断言（中文 11 + 英文 12，含边界 blank）——零额外 LLM 调用、不动 system 前缀冻结。实测：live refs 问题 → sources=[refs/crossrealm-realworld-notes.md] ✓；另：live 链路曾瞬时 AdvisorUnavailable（upstream-0），重试后 3/3 绿（HTTP 200，latency≈2.5 s）。W-Z244 — the Oracle now mechanically routes “real-world prototype” questions to the refs volume (creative/refs) with a keyword router — canon questions cite canon, prototype questions cite the reference notes, no cross-citation, zero extra LLM tokens, no cache-prefix freeze broken. 23 unit assertions (11 zh + 12 en + blank edge); live probe: refs question → sources=[refs/crossrealm-realworld-notes.md] ✓; upstream had one transient AdvisorUnavailable (upstream-0) and the retry was 3/3 green (HTTP 200, ≈2.5 s).
11 · 16第一百零五卷入典 · W-Z245 · 卷十五之六 · 仲冬见 《仲冬见》——仲冬前三天——下了一层薄雪——母亲磨了三天油纸伞——灶火安静得不寻常——第四天山坳里来了一个人——背篓高得快碰到头顶——白石头顶上的老人——他背着一个竹篓——竹篓里一个瓷罐祖灰和一枚铁丝勾着的火种——老人把那颗火种放进灶火——灶火跳了一下没熄——反而更亮了——七百年——两盏火——第一次在一个灶膛里对面上面下稳稳地烧——两守火人同时哭出来——小火喃喃念叨了些什么然后睡着了——老人走时留下一句话「祖火看见灶火了——灶火也看见祖火了」。Vol.105 · The Midwinter Visit — three days before midwinter, a light snow falls; his mother oils a paper umbrella and says nothing of why; on the fourth morning, a figure appears at the bend of the path — no horse, no cart, only one basket slung to his head; inside it, a porcelain jar of ancestor-ash, and a small ember held in a silk square in his robe; it is the old man from the mountain; he lays the ember into the stove; the stove leaps once — and does not diminish; both keepers weep without a sound; Xiao Huo whispers something no one can make out, then falls asleep at the stove’s foot; the old man is gone by dawn; the two remaining pieces of Bai Yin’s birch stand on either side of the stove as witnesses; that night no birch leapt in by itself — tonight there were two fires burning in one hearth.
11 · 16第一百零四卷入典 · W-Z242 · 卷十五之五 · 秋分 《秋分》——秋分这天雾散得比往年早——母亲起床时灶还没亮——她自己把火拢了起来——白桦码在灶台边白得像霜——灶火抖了一下然后稳稳地燃起来——小火醒来时灶膛里多了一根没人添的白桦——阿石蹲在灶前眼眶红了——「灶火自己添了」——第二天一家三口上山——白石头顶上那间小石屋——门口站着送信的那个人——两人隔着一面灶火跪下来——久久没说话——母亲从怀里掏出一包灶灰——他从怀里掏出祖火的灰——两种颜色肉眼分不出来——下山路上阿石背着小火——小火做了一个梦——梦见白茵站在山顶——同时看着两盏火——七百年——两盏火第一次见面。Vol.104 · The Autumn Equinox — on the day of the autumn equinox, the courtyard fog burns off earlier than it ever has; his mother rises before dawn and kindles the stove herself; the white birch splits pale as frost; the flame leaps once as if it recognizes something; Xiao Huo wakes to find a piece of white birch inside the firebox that nobody set there; A-Shi tells him the stove fed itself; the next morning the family climbs the mountain — at the top of the white stone ridge, a small stone house; the same man from the letter stands before it; two keepers kneel facing each other across the ridge and cannot speak; the stone bends the light back until all three are lit white-gold; his mother holds out a bag of stove-ash; the old man holds out a porcelain jar of ancestor-ash; they exchange them; the two colors cannot be told apart; Xiao Huo dreams of Bai standing on the ridge watching both fires at once; when he wakes, he is sleeping at the foot of the stove; the fire has climbed one inch higher.
11 · 16第一百零三卷入典 · W-Z241 · 卷十五之四 · 山顶来信 《山顶来信》——客人走后第十九天——又来一个人——白麻道袍袖口起毛——腰间一面抛光铜镜——麻袍连水都不肯喝——他只递来一封信——火漆压着一小块火苗印——切开——信很短——字迹瘦长笔直——和第一代守火人一模一样——「灶火烧得很好」——落款没有名字只有一个火苗印——母亲眼泪掉下来——小火问山顶上有没有灶——母亲说：七百年前——那块火先落在白茵手里——也先落在白石头顶上那间石头屋里——白茵的后人一路走到山顶守着祖火——六百年互不相见——六百年——六百年以来两家都还在烧——秋分——两边的火——要一起加白桦。Vol.103 · The Letter from the Peak — nineteen days after the guest has gone, another arrives; a white hemp robe whose cuffs have worn pale from a thousand washes; a polished copper mirror at the hip; he will not touch the water bowl; he hands over one letter sealed with wax pressed with a small flame; inside, a single page folded with a keeper’s precision; the hand is thin and upright — exactly like the first keeper’s; the message is one line: “You who keep the flame below — it is still burning there; we who keep this flame above — it is still burning here”; no name, only the same small flame; his mother weeps without sound; Xiao Huo asks whether there is a stove on the peak too; there is — a small stone house beside the white rock where the last of Bai Yin’s line has kept the ancestor-flame for seven hundred years; six hundred years of silence — and now, a letter; at the equinox, both fires will feed at once, on white birch; A-Shi cuts the one birch log in the house in half — one half feeds the stove tonight, the other waits in an old cloth for Xiao Huo to one day carry up the mountain alone.
11 · 16第一百零二卷入典 · W-Z239 · 卷十五之三 · 第一夜 《第一夜》——第七百年第十八天——小火学着阿石的样子码第一根柴——一根也不歪——等第三根柴码好他抬头时——阿石已经不再点头——第七百年第一个客从白石头方向来——走了一百二十二天——只为了看一眼灶火是不是真不灭——母亲端菜上桌——小火添柴阿石添水——那人从褡裢里掏出一面铁镜——镜面中央有铁线勾出的火苗——和灶前墙上小火画的那个圆圆火苗一样——母亲手指碰了碰镜面久久没说话——「炉边有碗——灶火在」——那人磕了一个头走了——七百年第一个回来的人——灶火那晚亮了一寸。Vol.102 · The First Night — on the eighteenth day of the seven-hundredth year, Xiao Huo learns to stack his first logs, straight and steady, and after three nods from A-Shi he no longer needs the nods; at dusk the first guest in remembered time arrives from the direction of the white stone — one hundred and twenty-two days on foot, just to see whether the stove really never goes out; his mother cooks, Xiao Huo feeds the fire, A-Shi pours the water; the traveler draws a small iron mirror from his bundle — a wire-drawn flame scratched into its face, the same round little flame Xiao Huo drew on the kitchen wall; he was sent by his mother, who no longer knew whether anyone still cared; A-Shi’s mother touches the wire flame and says nothing for a long time; “the stove is here,” she tells him; “reach home and feed one piece of wood into your own stove”; the traveler bows once — to the stove, to the tree, to the white stone — and is gone; inside, Xiao Huo weeps for the fire he did not yet know how to give; the fire burned one inch higher that night.
11 · 16第一百零一卷入典 · W-Z236 · 卷十五之二 · 传火（StateGraph 深章同步） 《传火》——第七百年的第五天——细雨一整天——母亲捧出一只锈皮铁匣——六百年没人按动暗扣——阿石按准——咔地一声——里面是一叠泛黄的纸和一块更旧的黑炭——第一代守火人的字瘦而稳——「火从白石头落下来——我接住了——它怕冷——只要还有人添柴它就不灭——传到第七百年的人还能看到这行字——灶火还在烧」——名字只剩半个偏旁——母亲说那是白茵——然后她看向小火：「落到他手里了」——阿石在纸上写下「续写」放回匣子——灶火亮了一寸——传火——不是传一团火——是传一段不灭的记忆。Vol.101 · The Handover — on the fifth day of the seven-hundredth year, a fine rain falls from morning to dusk; his mother sets a small iron box on the stove, fused by six hundred years of ash; A-Shi finds the hidden latch under the smoke, presses at exactly the right place; the lid opens with a click; inside is a stack of yellowed paper and one more piece of black charcoal, older than anything he has seen; the first keeper’s hand is narrow, straight, steady — “Fire fell from the white stone; I caught it; it is afraid of the cold; if anyone still feeds it, it never goes out”; the final signature burned away and his mother names the remaining half-radical: Bai Yin, the first keeper; “it fell into your hands,” she tells A-Shi — and “it falls into his today”; that evening one more page is laid into the box, in A-Shi’s two characters: continue writing; the fire climbs one inch in the dusk.
11 · 16说明书星图深章 · W-Z235 · hm_body_state_graph2 [H] 新增「星图 · StateGraph 内部机制」章节——逐行讲授 State / Node（patch merge by Object.assign）/ Edge 两类 / compile 静态良构 vs invoke 动态路由 / setMeta 标签 / trace 回放 / 节点纯度 副作用进 context——并给出 LangChain / LangGraph 生产 RAG 链路（retriever → reranker → generator → citation-gate + 证据不够回检索条件边）的完整映射——指明 src/core/StateGraph.js、src/content/fortunePath.js、src/game/FortunePathSystem.js、src/ui/FortunePathTab.js 与 tests/state-graph.test.js 25 用例——3225→3227 键、helpSectionsExtra 10→11——全量 6754/6754 绿。W-Z235 — the [H] manual gains “Star Map · Under the Hood of StateGraph”, a line-by-line walkthrough of State / Node patch-merge / the two kinds of Edge / compile-time static shapeCheck vs invoke-time dynamic routing / setMeta / trace replay / node purity — with a complete LangChain/LangGraph production mapping (retriever → reranker → generator → citation-gate plus the “not enough evidence → back to retrieval” conditional edge), pointing at src/core/StateGraph.js, src/content/fortunePath.js, src/game/FortunePathSystem.js, src/ui/FortunePathTab.js and the 25 cases in tests/state-graph.test.js; i18n 3225→3227, helpSectionsExtra 10→11, full suite 6754/6754 green.
11 · 16第100卷入典 · W-Z234 · 卷十五 · 第七百年 · 百卷里程碑 《第百卷》——灶火燃了六百年七百年——灶火从来没伏——第九十九回落笔——阿石站在灶前——火种树挺直在晨光里——院里——小火拉了拉阿石的袖子——阿石把手放进灶火——灶火吃他的指尖——六百年——六百年守火人的最后一站。Vol.100 · The Hundredth Volume — the hundred-volume canon milestone; the stove has burned for seven hundred years without missing a single morning; A-Shi places one palm on the iron; the fire warms him the way it has always warmed; six hundred years of keepers sit behind him in that moment — all of them present, all of them looking forward; the fire burns steadily; the fire-seed tree in the garden sways with the wind; a thousand keeper-generations ahead of them — and the stove will be there to greet them all.
11 · 16九十九卷入典 · W-Z233 · 卷十四之五·抵达 《抵达》——火种树的针叶整棵向东弯去——灶火第一次明白了自己等六百年——它弓着腰——同一棵火种树弓了六百年——阿石把手按在树干上——它是暖的——暖透了它六百年——祖火灶火互相映应——那两个火六百年第一次对上了——第七百年——两束光——树弓弯——灶弓稳稳——灶火抵达了。Vol.99 · The Arrival — in the days after the gleam, every needle of the fire-seed tree in the courtyard begins to lean eastward, toward the same ridge where it all began; they have been leaning since six hundred years ago; he places his palm on the trunk and feels its warmth; his mother says: “the tree has remembered the first keeper — fire from the white stone six hundred years ago — they always were bowing — we just didn’t see them until now”; east, over the ridge, past six hundred years of waiting, two distant gleams finally meet each other.
11 · 16九十八卷入典 · W-Z232 · 卷十四之四·阁楼 《阁楼》——阿石听见头顶阁楼上有很轻的响动——他上去看——里面空空的——桌上有一块煤——煤很普通——只有晨光里——它炉心还在隐隐地亮——他把煤捧在手心——白纸压在煤底下——纸上只有一行字：「灶火把一切都带走」——阿石把它送进灶火——整间厨房亮了起来——灶火烧得更稳——火种树在院子里摇了三下。Vol.98 · The Loft — A-Shi hears a soft shift above; he climbs; empty room, thick dust, a coal on a wobbly table; pale morning light reveals its faint warm glow; a small slip of paper beneath: “the fire carries everything forward”; warm to the touch; he carries it downstairs; the stove eats it in a single silent breath; the kitchen fills with gentle white-gold light; outside, the fire-seed tree sways in the warm light; morning begins.
11 · 16九十七卷入典 · W-Z231 · 卷十四之三·光里 《光里》——阿石一梦刚醒灶火一亮——厨房间从天亮到晚亮没停过——母亲光着脚走出去——她退一步但跪在灶前——灶火亮起六百年厨房——她伸出手——灶火把她双手含住——他们都湿了脸——六百年的守火魂六百年正午那一束真正的光——阿石也走进去——火碰到他——暖得像树叶间隔的凉——他慢慢跪在灶前——灶火六百年问他——他的手烫吗——灶火没有烫——六百年。Vol.97 · In the Light — A-Shi wakes before dawn into a light that had no source; the stove glows with a white-gold radiance; his mother steps out barefoot, her hair uncombed; she kneels by the stove; she puts both hands into the light; it does not burn her; she weeps silently, her face lit; A-Shi reaches his hand in beside hers; the warmth enters him fingertips-to-chest, settling like a heartbeat finally finding its rhythm; outside, the day begins; two lights meet on the kitchen floor and neither cancels the other.
11 · 16九十六卷入典 · W-Z230 · 卷十四之二·白茵 《白茵》——晌午白茵忽然来了——没带行李——灶火认出她就舔了一下——她伸手靠近灶火——灶火跳一下——母亲端一碗水来——白茵把它浇在灶火上——灶火跳成白色——她走到灶前说「灶火会继续燃——灶火从此往下传」——她走了——灶火更亮——院子里火种树抖了一下——册序已经准备好——她回来了。Vol.96 · Baiyin — Baiyin arrives at midday without warning; the stove sees her and rises in a welcoming curve; his mother brings water; Baiyin pours it into the stove the way generations before her have, and it flares white-gold; she stands by the stove and says: “the fire will burn on — the fire will pass down”; she leaves; the stove burns even brighter; the fire-seed tree shivers.
11 · 16九十五卷入典 · W-Z229 · 卷十四《第七百年》开篇·续火 《续火》——白茵三个月后的回信——信纸发着暖——什么也没写——但六百年笔墨已落在里面——灶火继续写着——它照常燃，白奶奶照常站——灶火没有停过，也不会停——第七百年——它自己收的火。Vol.95 · The Following Fire — Volume XIV “The Seven-Hundredth Year” opens: a letter arrives almost empty, warm to the touch; his mother walks in and knows at once what it is; she draws out a small skillet from the hearth-shelf, the iron still warm from six hundred years of cooking, of keeping; Xiao Huo toddles over and nestles the skillet against his grandfather; the stove burned steady as ever; the seven-hundredth year has begun — right there in the kitchen — and the stove does not stop.
11 · 16九十四卷入典 · W-Z228 · 卷十三之六·母亲灶 《母亲灶》——阿石清早下楼的时候，母亲在灶前跪——她先动手——六百年女子每天先点燃最后那块炭——每一只前面那一辈都在她脸上过——她用米糠轻轻往灶火吹——六百年的话没人听——灶火一直听见——阿石坐在灶前慢慢懂了——灶火不是家具——灶火才是她们每个人的名字。Vol.94 · The Mother’s Stove — A-Shi comes down before dawn; his mother is already kneeling at the stove, feeding a pinch of rice husk into the flame; six hundred years of women who stood before this same hearth — no one ever spoke of them by name, the fire did, every single morning, forever; the stove cracks contentedly; the house breathes with the stove’s rhythm; the fire is the first act of every day; the fire is every morning of every life before his.
11 · 16九十三卷入典 · W-Z227 · 卷十三之五·夜 《夜》——小火第一次独自添柴——他伸手按在灶膛的铁板上——一缕细细的热风吹上他的手——他觉得到了灶火自己的心跳——他把睡着了的阿石扶在灶沿上——他知道这六百年，灶火一直都在守着这间家——整夜窗外夜风吹过——灶火始终没灭。Vol.93 · The Night — Xiao Huo feeds fire for the first time alone; a thin warm breath touches his palm and he feels the stove’s very heart; he rocks his sleeping grandfather quietly against the stove’s iron ledge; the stove never goes out; the fire-seed’s every leaf leans toward the kitchen in the dark; six hundred years of keeping, held one breath at a time.
11 · 16九十二卷入典 · W-Z226 · 卷十三之四·灶火 《灶火》——春末灶火燃了六百年——母亲清晨把手伸进灶膛——伸出一块拇指大小的灰白色炭——六百年前守火人越不过白石的那粒灶火——她把那块炭放进了小火手里——小火接过来没烫——灶火——整间屋亮了——绿了六百年的山上——心照不误——第七百年——真正开始了。Vol.92 · The Hearth — late spring; six hundred years of the stove’s fire; her mother reaches into the stove and draws out a thumb-sized coal, grey-white and utterly ancient; the coal that was kindled at the mountain’s white stone six hundred years ago finally descends into the little hand that has been waiting for it; the stove glows gold and settles, content and steady; the whole courtyard glistens; the seventh century of keeping is real.
11 · 16九十一卷入典 · W-Z225 · 卷十三之三·送礼 《送礼》——母亲早晨塞给阿石一个巴掌大的小稻草盒——盒子里什么也没有——他和小火背着空盒子上路——驿站里白茵坐在灶前——她接过空盒子，轻轻一合，笑了：「这六百年的礼，今天算是收下了。」——灶火传过来又传回去——六百年的楔子——灶火没把火带远——它就在那儿——第六百一十二位守火人像第六百一十二位守火人那样，稳稳地烧着。Vol.91 · The Bestowal — spring; his mother hands A-Shi and Xiao Huo a tiny straw-woven bundle with nothing inside it; they carry it up the mountain; at the relay Baiyin sits by her stove; she opens the empty bundle and smiles — “the fire has finally been received; it has decided not to return”; the empty box itself is the gift — the gift six hundred years of keepers finally got to give back.
11 · 16九十卷入典 · W-Z224 · 卷十三之二·墨 《墨》——小火最后一页翻页——六百年灶火在他落笔的那一刻一起跳了一下——那是一个没人看见过的字：「火」——白奶奶的影子一瞬间就过去了——「他们都在这里」——火——保持着——六百年没灭。Vol.90 · Ink — on the winter solstice itself, Xiao Huo takes the brush at last; the word the fire itself taught him is written; the moment the stroke lands, all six hundred years of the fire-family leap at once; a thin silhouette passes through the heart of the flame, an ancient figure unbearably patient — she looks at Xiao Huo once, then at A-Shi, and is gone; “they are all here,” Xiao Huo says; the ledger closes; the stove keeps watching.
11 · 16八十九卷入典 · W-Z223 · 卷十三《新火·第七百年》开篇 《新火》——小火在阿石家守灶第一个月——每天只教他看灶火——第一天他说「火在动」——第二周他说「里面有人轮流守火」——第三周他说「他们跟我一样，都是守火的人」——母亲从后面看见这个孩子背后有一束白光——那是六百年前第一代守火人看着的那种——册序翻开他还没写——册子说：「下一步我写」——火种树叶子变成了深红——卷十二落幕——卷十三：第七百年真正开始。Vol.89 · New Fire — Volume XIII “New Fire: The Seven-Hundred Year” opens the next saga; Xiao Huo spends his first month at A-Shi’s tending the stove; day by day his answers grow: “The fire moves” → “There are people taking turns inside” → “They all look like me, like every keeper before me”; his mother glimpses a spectral thread of light clinging to his shoulders; the ledger is opened before him; he does not write yet — the hearth waits — the fire-seed tree’s needles turn deepest crimson; the beacon’s smoke has lifted from the ridge; the seven-hundredth year begins.
11 · 16八十八卷入典 · W-Z222 《新人》——雪后那一天，八九岁的光头孩子站在灶火前——衣着破旧——他手里攥着一粒冷的黑石头——「我是小火」——阿石懂了他是谁——他是白奶奶托人送来的第六百零二位——两枚黑煤在灶沿上碰面——整片山谷的烟囱一起亮了起来。Vol.88 · The Newcomer — a boy stands in the doorway, eight or nine years old, head shaved, robes washed to the colour of ash; “I am small fire”; A-Shi understands at once — Grandma Bai sent him; the coal he carries in his sleeve meets the one A-Shi has kept all this time; the two pieces of fire touch — every stove in the valley answers at once; the family arc holds one breath.
11 · 16八十七卷入典 · W-Z221 《白奶奶》——灶火第三天整白光发亮——册子里夹着包布——白茵笔迹：「白奶奶是火，不归人，归炉」——六百年每一位守火人喊的“白奶奶”，其实是叫火本身——灶火接受了。Vol.87 · The Grandmother — A-Shi finds a tiny yellowed strip on the table with Baiyin’s handwriting — three characters: “Grandmother Bai” — the fire was calling the fire’s own daughter by her name, and six hundred years of keepers had answered without knowing why; the stove settles, gentle as a grandmother tucking her grandson in.
11 · 16八十六卷入典 · W-Z220 《灯》——白茵从山上来，屋里的光从来没有灭过一秒——「你的信交出去了吗？」——「册子快满了。」——她开门走出去时灶火蹿起一记整红整白的高火，屋里亮得透明——六百年里每一位守火人的脸都翻升一瞬——信收走了——册子最后一页安静地空空——第六百零二位守火人——已经准备好了。Vol.86 · The Lamp — Baiyin comes down from the mountain; the kitchen light never goes out for a single second; “have you given it to them yet?” she asks; “the ledger is nearly full”; as she steps out, the stove flares with a white-gold flame so clear that six hundred years of keepers’ faces appear lit by the same fire they kept; the letter is gone; the final page of the ledger is calm and blank — the next keeper’s page, ready for the hand that will write it.
11 · 16八十五卷入典 · W-Z219 《信》——冬至后第三天阿石决定给白茵写信——他划掉六百年所有字——只写了四个字：「灶火，不熄」——母亲看见没问——子夜灶火跳了一下册子自动翻开——册子最后一页出现新的字：他爷爷的字——「被封的书，会自己醒过来」——第二天那页上只剩白茵的字——他把信重新放到灶前——六百年的火，稳稳地烧下去。Vol.85 · The Letter — the third day after the Solstice A-Shi decides to write to Baiyin; he strikes out every stroke — and finally writes four characters: “The fire will not go out”; past midnight the stove leaps and the ledger opens itself — on its final page appears a line in his grandfather’s hand: “A sealed book wakes itself”; the next morning only Baiyin’s words remain — the letter sealed in the stove-edge, and the fire burning steadier than ever.
11 · 16八十四卷入典 · W-Z218 《结木》——阿石冬至晨出去添柴，看见院子里那棵火种树上结了一枚果子——母亲说火种树第一枚果子是接火的信——子夜过后他看见灶前坐着一位陌生人——他是阿石家的守火家谱——册序——白奶奶的六百页——白奶奶说下一册也快了——第三枚果子落下雪地整谷亮了——卷十二·远行——收束于下一站。Vol.84 · The Cone — Winter Solstice morning, A-Shi sees the first cone on the fire-seed tree; his mother says this is the sign the next keeper is on the road; past midnight he wakes to a grey-robed stranger at the stove — he has brought the sixth-hundred-and-second keeper’s ledger — every page his ancestors wrote — and one blank page; the snow falls all night; the third cone drops into the snow; the whole valley answers.
11 · 16八十三卷入典 · W-Z217 《册》——阿石写下第一笔「记」——册子里六百年守火人的笔迹在他眼前摊开——他在最后一张空白页写下自己的「记」——横是他爷爷的横，竖是他爸爸的竖，墨是他自己的——白茵说：第六百零二位守火人从这一页开始——这本册子不是合上，是翻开。Vol.83 · The Ledger — A-Shi turns the final blank page of the six-hundred-year ledger and writes his own 记 — his grandfather’s short-across stroke, his father’s lighter line, his own ink — Baiyin names him the six-hundred-and-second keeper; this book does not close, it opens.

11 · 16卷十二 · 第八十一卷 第二盏灯 · W-Z213 《第二盏灯》——阿石到了第二盏灯：灯前坐着个约他年纪的女人，她从衣袋里掏出跟他一模一样的木牌——她是白奶奶的妹妹——南头熄北头接、一边走一边把这盏南头风传了几百年——她给他一枚六百年没动用的火种——第三盏灯最后一刻被点燃：紫白天边上三盏黄灯连成一串光——六百年第一次。Vol.81 · The Second Lamp — A-Shi reaches the second lamp on the ash-flat; a young woman tends the straw-fed fire; she draws from her sleeve a wooden plaque twin to his — “the fire has come this far” — she is Grandma Bai’s sister; the south’s flame has been riding the north wind for six hundred years to keep this generation of lamps lit; she hands him a fresh coal from the six-hundred-year cache; at dusk the third lamp is lit from burning straw, and the three lamps burn in line across the northern flatland — the first time in six hundred years.
11 · 16卷十二 · 第八十卷 北灯 · W-Z211 《北灯》——卷十二《远行 —— 火与墨》开篇：翻过烟囱岭，阿石看见北边有三盏与灶火同样六百年的灯；第一盏前坐着一个比母亲还老的老人——他等了阿石六百年零一日；北风告诉他们南边一直有人烧着一团不熄的火；三灯连成一条光，闪过了六百年。《守火——守到全案》。Vol.80 · The North Lamp — Volume XII “The Long Journey: Fire and Ink” opens: north of the ridge three lamps glow, six hundred years light side by side — the first keeper sits by the first lamp, his mother’s age, and he has waited six hundred and one days; the north wind has been carrying A-Shi’s stove-fire’s scent; the three lamps light at once — for the first time in six hundred years — a horizon lit end to end.
11 · 16七十九卷入典 · W-Z210 《出山》——灶火的心绪像六百年走到头的一口气；白茵站在门口：「你该出山了」——往北三盏灯与你家灶火同样六百年——山里守火人守灶，山外守火人传火——阿石背上祖父行囊走出院门——灶火跳了一记——六百年第一次，守火人迈出了山门。Vol.79 · Into the Outlands — the stove’s heartbeat has shifted; Baiyin stands in the doorway and says — you must leave the mountain — three lamps burning for six hundred years beyond the ridge, same as this hearth — two callings: valley keeper tends the stove; outlands keeper carries the fire — A-Shi steps through the gate for the first time in six hundred years — and the stove gives one low, bright leap.
11 · 16七十八卷入典 · W-Z209 《客》——一个七岁小孩翻山越岭向阿石借火：一只鞋穿一只没穿，递上一张画着一双粗大的眼睛的纸，不说话；白奶奶托他来的——灶是她奶奶家灭了——第六百年里第一次有人来借火；阿石把小纸包里的煤浸了灶灰水——它蓝起来——白奶奶托他带一句：「火种会自己走路」——守火到这就不是一个人的了。Vol.78 · The Guest — a seven-year-old girl crosses the ridge alone to borrow fire for her grandmother’s dead stove — one shoe on, one off, a wordless drawn pair of eyes in her hand — Grandma Bai sent her — the first guest in six hundred years; A-Shi dips her coal into stove-ash and watches it flame blue — she leaves with a message from Grandma Bai: “the fire-seed walks on its own feet.”
11 · 16七十七卷入典 · W-Z208 《碎石》——雷劈开白石头之后，阿石伸手进石缝，摸到一粒没烧透的煤：六百年前第一个守火人从来没有点成的那一粒火种——雷没法拆开它，只让它落进了他的掌心；他带回灶边父亲的木板，凿了一个半通不通的洞——那一夜罐子里的煤亮了；火种树顶端的针叶比从前还深红。Vol.77 · The Broken Stone — inside the cracked white stone, A-Shi finds the first coal, unlit for six hundred years by the first keeper’s own hand; lightning could not destroy it — it only let it fall into his palm; he seals it into an iron canister on a groove in his father’s workbench; that night the coal glows; the seed-tree’s tip needle turns a deeper red.
11 · 16七十六卷入典 · W-Z207 《雷火》——六百年从未灭过的白石头守夜灯被一道紫白闪电劈灭；阿石在雷雨顶上冲上山、顺着半山腰那股松香风跑到村东废弃滩涂——一团六百年的蓝火在松林首领手里燃烧，他泼上最后一勺灶灰水，蓝焰转金，第六百零一位守火人叫住了那团火：它折进他怀里的木盒——他家的灶更旺了，火种树整夜抖动。Vol.76 · Thunderfire — a violet-white bolt cracks the white stone; the beacon dies after six hundred years; A-Shi rides the lightning-wind to the abandoned pine flats — a six-hundred-year blue flame waiting in the Lord of the Pines; he pours the last spoonful of stove-ash on it and it turns to gold — the fire comes home into his little wooden breast-box; the stove burns higher; the yard seed-tree flickers through the night.
11 · 16七十五卷入典 · W-Z206 《记》——六百零二位守火人门前的册子上，还剩最后一个空页；每天一个「记」字的练习，第五片针穿出火种树——阿石决定「等树长成了再写」。Vol.75 · The Character — the ledger of the keepers has one page left; daily practice of 记 (to remember); the fire-seed tree grows its fifth needle — A-Shi decides: when the tree is grown, he will write his 记.

六个问题的答案Six AnswersDELIBERATELY NARROW

为什么用浏览器

Why browser

因为《跨界纪元》不落卡——点一下就进、不要注册、不要下载。最低配置电脑能跑满帧。

Because the game is the door: click, play, done. No install, no signup, regular hardware runs at 60.

为什么全双平台语言

Why full bilingual

因为火所有语言都听懂。所有文案双表维护 · 死键/缺键门禁 const play at CI.

Because the flame listens in every language. All strings live in both locales; missing-key gates fail CI.

为什么玩家不违规

Why no cheats

自动通关是给讲故事用的，不是给刷榜用的——榜单不接自动通关。

Autoplay is for storytelling, never for ranks — leaderboards exclude autoplay runs.

为什么 17 退

Why 17

七章 · 三 Boss · 一百一十回声 · 四十成就 · 十七母题最后一层。退开就有 17 个抱歉在门后等你。

Seven chapters · three bosses · 110 echoes · 40 achievements · the seventeenth mother in the last row. There’s a thing waiting at the end of 17.

为什么 110

Why 110

一百十分线的没出现，事情还没完——你比想象的结局更多一格。

The hundred-and-tenth is missing — there is always one more, one ahead of you, one beyond the wall.

为什么未完

Why “open end”

因为结束在「余烬」之后仍有一层纸条：「此驿站不收信。路过的人，下次再坐。」

Because even after the ending: the notice board reads “We take no letters here — only travellers, come sit a while.”
